## LOI NOI DAU

San xuat xay dung la mot nganh san xuat cong nghiep chiem ti trong lon ngan sach quoc gia. De viec sit dung von dau tu vao cac cong trinh co hieu qua, nhitng nguoi lam cong tac xay dung khong nhitng phai luon luon doi moi cong nghe san xuat, chon bien phap thi cong tien tien ma con phai biet to chitc san xuat mot cach khoa hoc. Doi moi cong nghe, thay doi bien phap thi cong doi hoi ta dau tu them tien von, con to chitc san xuat khoa hoc doi hoi ta tim toi sang tao, van dung kien thitc vao san xuat. Vi vay nhieu khi ta tim duoc mot phuong an to chitc tot se mang lai hieu qua kinh te gap nhieu lain so voi dau tu tien von. Dac biet doi voi nhiing nen san xuat lon nhu xay dung, muon thang loi khong the khong quan tam den cong tac to chitc, dièu hanh san xuat. Qua trinh san xuat cang phitc tap thi to chitc dieu hanh cang phai tinh vi chinh xac. Muon vay nguoi quan ly cong viec ngoai kinh nghiem tich luy khong nhitng phai nam vitng nhitng quy trinh quy pham cing nhu nhitng phap lenh cia nha nuoc ve quan ly dau tu xay dung ma con can thong hieu kie'n thitc khoa hoc ve chuyen nganh to' chitc xay dung.

Do nhu cau cia thuc te san xuat va nghien ciu giang day ? nha truong mot cach he thong ly thuyet co ban ve "T6' chuc san xuat xay dung', chung toi bien soan bo sach TO CHUC XAY DUNG. Sach duoc viet thanh hai tap, tap mot chuyen ve lap ke' hoach, to' chitc va dieu hanh san xuat. Tap hai chuyen ve thiet ke' va to' chitc mat bang thi cong.

## KHAI NIEM VE CHUYEN NGANH TO CHUC XAY DUNG


## 1.1. NHIEm vy vA mUC diCh CUa ChUyEn NGANh to ChUC XAY DUNG

Xay dung la mot nganh san xuat cd ban trong nen kinh te quoc dan. Xay dung dong vai tro quan trong trong xay dung cd s? vat chat, ky thuat cua xa x a theo dinh huong xa hoi chu nghia nen co vi tri dac biet quan trong. San pham te, nhat la trong giai doan phat trien ban dau. Xay dung la bieu hien su phat trien cua mot xa hoi. Chinh vi le d6 Nha nuoc ta coi xay dung la mot trong ban vi san pham cua no la tai san cua xa hoi gop phan lam ra nhung san pham khac.

suoa wgiyo sunp uep ba datyau gugo sunp Aex tya uea po gunp Aex guoi lon. Nhiem vu cua nhung nguoi xay dung la phai su dung co hieu qua von dau tu de dat duoc muc dich de ra trong giai doan ngan nhat.

Nham muc dich tren nguoi ta phai van dung sang tao nhung thanh qua cua tien bo khoa hoc, ky thuat, cong nghe, khai thac tiem nang tu nhien va co s? vat chat ky thuat cua cac don vi san xuat. Tat ca dieu d6 phu thuoc vao trinh do t6 chuc san xuat cua nguoi dieu hanh trong qua trinh xay dung cong trinh.

Nhu ta da biet xay dung dan dung va cong nghiep la tap hop nhieu qua trinh san xuat phuc tap lien quan den nhieu nganh san xuat khac nhau. Viec dam bao su phoi hop giua cac qua trinh mot cach nhip nhang, dat hieu qua la mot bai toan phuc tap, doi hoi nguoi ky su xay dung phai biet ly thuyet he thong u unp uea ei eryan sunp Aex aenx ues onuo ga deyd suonyd ga soy eouy np uo o e p o hip p oo o o a chat ky thuat hoac tai nguyén.

Tom lai nhiém vu cua mon hoc to chuc xay dung la hoan thien he thong quan ly, xac dinh cac phuong phap to chic, chi dao xay dung mot cach khoa

## Muc dich co ban cua to' chuc san xuat xay dung la:

- 1) Xay dung cong trinh dung thdi' han da duoc cac van ban phap lyan dinh nhu: ké hoach cua Nha nuoc neu cong trinh nha nuoc chi dao, hop dong giao nhan thau neu la cong trinh thuc hien theo hop dong A-B, quyet dinh cua chu dau tu neu la cong trinh tu lam, hay uy thac xay dung.... Moi su rut ngan hay kéo dai thoi gian thi cong deu phai xu ly thuong phat nghiem minh phu hop voi hieu qua hoac thua thiet ve kinh te.
- 2) Bao dam nang xuat lao dong cao cua tat ca cac t6 chuc tham gia xay lap. Dieu nay n6i lén tinh cong dong hop tac cung co loi nhat trong lién doanh san xuat.
- 3) Bao dam chat luong cao cho cong trinh. Chat luong the hien muc dat cac tieu chuan ky thuat cua cac cong viec xay lap do thiet ke quy dinh. auop doy ueq uea auo lua oonp gunp de suoni teyo uenyo nan oeo Muon bao dam chat luong ngudi xay dung phai hoan thien he thong quan ly chat luong (vi du nhu ISO 9ooo) dudc thi truong cong nhan va duoc tu van giam sat dong y.
- 4) Dat hieu qua kinh te cao. D6 la vüa dat tieu chuan chat luong ky thuat vua co gia thanh ha. Day la dieu kien de t6 chuc xay lap ton tai va phat trién.

T6 chuc xay lap c6 kha nang dat duoc nhung nhung muc dich tren moi co du suc canh tranh trén thi truong trong nuoc cung nhu quoc te, khi co che thi Aex danuau queop oeo eno guep qura touo ues zou nuu sunp dy opnp gupnn dung.

Kha nang va suc canh tranh cua don vi xay lap the hien nang luc san sang thuc hien nhung dieu kien cua thi truong san xuat cu the la:

- San sang trien khai san xuat theo quy mo cong trinh nhan thau (chu dong trien khai cong viec).
- C6 san co so vat chat ky thuat (nang luc trang thiet bi, doi ngu ky su, chuyen gia va cong nhan lanh nghe tuong thich voi cong trinh).
- Lua chon phuong an cong nghe xay lap hop ly.
- Bao dam cung ung tai nguyen can thiet cho san xuat.
- Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS

Khoa hoc to chuc xay dung luon gan lién voi kien thuc cua nhieu chuyén mon dac biet ve cong nghe xay dung, co gioi hoa xay dung va kinh te xay dung. D6 la nhung kien thuc tien quyet nguoi ky su xay dung phai nam vung truoc khi bat tay vao cong viec lap ke hoach to chuc va dieu hanh san xuat xay dung.


## 1.2. dAC dIEm CUA CHUYEN NGANH SAN XUAT XAY DUNG

Xay dung cung nhu cac nganh nghe san xuat cong nghiep khac, n6 co nhung quy luat chung va co nhung dac diem rieng. O day ta chi neu va phan tich nhung dac diem c6 lien quan nhieu den hieu qua cua hoat dong san xuat xay dung.

- 1) San pham cua san xuat xay dung la nhung cong trinh, ket tinh tu cac thanh qua khoa hoc - cong nghe, la ket qua cua nhieu nganh, nhieu to chuc kinh té xa hoi, d6 la chu dau tu, cac doanh nghiep xay lap, cac cd quan tu van thiet ke, cac cong ty cung ung vat tu thiet bi, cac to chuc dich vu ngan hang, tai chinh va cac co quan quan ly cua Nha nuoc. Diéu d6 cho ta thay muon mot du an thanh cong tot phai co su phoi hop lien nganh, lién cd quan va luon nam dudi su quan ly cua Nha nuoc.
- 2) Cong trinh xay dung thuong c6 von dau tu lon chiem ti trong lon trong ngan sach quoc gia. Vi vay chien luoc trong dau tu xay dung luon la trong diem cua nha nuoc.

![Page 4](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/25e07fdc-9703-4107-aed1-6c70926cfab6.png)

---

Chi tinh riéng phan xay lap nhung cong trinh binh thuong, nh? cung vai tram trieu lon toi hang chuc ti dong. Chua ke den nhung cong trinh dac biet, trong diem.

- 3) San xuat xay dung luon gan lien voi su phat trien cua cac nganh kinh te va muc song cua nhan dan. Khi kinh te xa hoi phat trien kéo theo san xuat xay dung phat trien dac biet trong linh vuc xay dung cong nghiep va dan dung.
- 4) Trong san xuat xay dung nguoi dau tu va nguoi su dung san pham khong phai la ngudi thuc hien xay dung. Trong d6 luon ton tai nhung hop dong A - B va luon can hoat dong tu van, giam sat.
- 5) San pham xay dung la nhung cong trinh gan lien voi dia diem nhat dinh (bat dong san) do d6 san xuat xay dung chiu su tac dong cua yeu to dia phuong. Dia diem san xuat luon thay doi. Luc luong san xuat luon phai di dong, co cau t6 chuc xay lap luon thay doi cho phu hop voi cong trinh lam tang nhung chi phi gian tiep.

- 6) San xuat xay dung mang tinh xa hoi va thay d6i theo thoi gian n6 chiu anh huong cua phong tuc, tap quan, trinh do van hoa va quan diem cua ngudi si dung.
- 7) Thoi gian xay dung mot cong trinh tuong doi dai ? ngoai trdi nen chiu tac dong cua thdi tiet, thi trudng lén qua trinh san xuat kha ro ret. Viec a oex yuo uop sueu uonn ryd tou lop sunp Aex uaty ony onuo 9 nhay cam, doi khi con co mot phan may rui.
- 8) Qua trinh san xuat xay dung la tap hop nhieu qua trinh thanh phan, moi qua trinh co nhieu phuong án ky thuat va t6 chuc. De di den phudng an tot nhat nguoi dieu hanh san xuat phai khang dinh duoc nhung phuong an kha thi de tim ra phuong an hop ly lam co s? chon phuong an toi uu. Qua trinh chon phuong an ta phai tien hanh nhung so sanh theo nhung tieu chi de ra. Day la viec lam doi h?i nguoi can bo phai co trinh do, nang luc va nhieu kinh nghiem trong san xuat.

hién duoc. Phuong an hop ly la phuong án kha thi nhung phai phu hop v6i dieu kien thuc te thi cong. Dieu kién thi cong la kha nang cua don vi xay lap, vi tri xay dung cong trinh va nhung dieu kien khac ma no bi rang buoc (theo hop dong, quy uoc...). Phuong án toi uu la phuong án sunp Aex yonu eu yyo nan sunqu oaun aequ oo na yyo oeo oo Ai doy de ra (thoi gian, gia thanh, tai nguyen...)

- 9) San xuat xay dung su dung nhieu lao dong song, ma lao dong lai la uoni oni ueyu fi doy gunp ns ugon ieq uau waty fnb uanau yea uonau dat ra voi moi phuong an thi cong.

## 1.3. HUONG PHAT TRIEN CUA CHUYEN NGANH SAN XUAT XAY DUNG

Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS Nhu ta da khang dinh xay dung la mot trong nhung chuyen nganh khoa hoc ky thuat ung dung. Moi tien bo khoa hoc, ky thuat deu tac dong len su phat a a o a oo qu oo a op 'up Ax hnx us eno u liéu xay dung. Co nhung bien phap ky thuat da duoc ly thuyet dua ra kha lau nhung chi khi co thiet bi tuong ung no moi tr? thanh mot cong nghe san xuat moi. Doi voi su xuat hien mot loai vat lieu moi cung co ket qua nhu vay.

Chung ta co the hieu mot cong nghe la mot mo hinh san xuat trong d6 bao gom nguyen vat lieu, thiet bi va mot quy trinh san xuat. Nhu vay mot trong cac yeu t6 thay d6i ta c6 mot cong nghe moi. Ngugc lai muon hinh thanh mot dou nyd iop rea ooy 9 ep auu suoo eou ep uau rqd 1 iou auu suo hon voi dieu kien thi truong san xuat hien hanh.

aunp Kex eno oa ua auony yunuy oeuy henx ues queau oo nyu sun khong ngung d6i moi cong nghe, cong nghiep hoa hien dai hoa nen san xuat. Tat ca muc tieu do duoc the hien qua hai huong chinh la luon ap dung nhung tién bo khoa hoc ky thuat vao san xuat va doi moi phuong thuc quan ly. Cu the la:

## a) Co gioi hoa dong bo cac qua trinh san xuat

'o K ua un o u uno n up on uno 1 eo p gi6i h6a la cot loi cua cong nghiép hoa. Co gi6i hoa nham giai ph6ng lao dong chan tay, lam tang nang suat lao dong va tieu chuan hoa duoc chat luong. Muon phat huy duoc hieu qua cua may moc thi co gi6i hoa phai dong bo, dong bo theo cong nghe, theo nang suat va chat luong.

np ieyd dgl Kex ia upp oq sugp eoy o po nan onu sonp uaty onya uonn manh ve kinh te va ky thuat, c6 tiem nang doi dao de mua sam thiet bi, hoac thuc hien lien doanh lien ket giua cac don vi xay dung. Doi voi Nha nuoc la su sat nhap nhieu don vi nh? thanh nhung cong ty lon, doi voi quoc te la su lien doanh giua cong ty trong nuoc va cac doi tac nuoc ngoai.


## b) Tu dong hoa san xuat

Tu dong hoa la hinh thuc cao cua co gioi hoa. No giup nang cao nang suat lao dong va lam mat di ranh gioi giua lao dong tri 6c va chan tay. Muc do tu dong hoa phu thuoc vao tung cong nghe san xuat. Hien nay trong xay dung tu dong hoa con rat thap ngay ca nhung nuoc cong nghiep tien tien.

Khuynh huong chung cua san xuat cong nghiep la tien toi cang nhieu tu dong hoa cang tot nhat la nhung cong viec lam trong moi truong doc hai va nang nhoc.

Truoc tien tu dong hoa nham vao nhung cong viec da dugc cd gioi hoa va trong linh vuc lap ke hoach, quan ly va dieu hanh san xuat. Bieu hien la viéc ap dung may tinh dien tu vao dieu hanh mot so quy trinh cong nghe, quan ly du an va dieu hanh san xuat.


## c) Cong nghiep hoa nganh xay dung

Song song v6i co gioi h6a mot khuynh huong lon la cong nghiép h6a nganh xay dung. Cong nghiep hoa la dua nhung cong viéc ngoai hién truong vao u u up uno Ae equ unx suoo gunu uo uny oa hoa se dua san xuat xay dung gan voi cac nganh san xuat cong nghiep khac; lam giam su vat va cua cong nhan khi lam viec ngoai troi; lam han che anh huong cua thdi tiet den qua trinh san xuat. Cong nghiep hoa cang cao thi nang suat, chat luong lao dong cang lon, nang cao hieu qua su dung may moc tao dieu kién cho cd gioi hoa va tu dong hoa phat trien. Dac biet trong xay dung cong nghiep, cong nghiep hoa khong the khong quan tam, do n6 con co y nghia khu vuc va quoc te vi dac thu b?i nhung thiet ke va cong nghe dien hinh.

![Page 6](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/6a061ec1-cdf4-485e-883b-15df2cb4e8ee.png)

## d) Sir dung toi da ket cau lap ghep

Trong dieu kien co the nen su dung toi da cac ket cau lap ghép, vi day la Suoo ouo uai nap oia daua dgi sunp dy 'un uan sunp dex gyau suoo aou nghiep hoa phat huy uu the. Lap ghép giup rut ngan thoi gian thi cong, tiet kiem duoc cop pha, han che tac dong cua thdi tiet. Trong ket cau thép doi khi lap ghép la phuong an duy nhat va toi uu. Muon cong nghe lap ghep phat huy the manh phai dau tu nhieu vao khau nghien cuu thiet ke kien truc, ket cau va tim toi bien phap thi cong co hieu qua.


## e) Sit dung vat lieu mbi thay the vat lieu truyen thong, khong nging doi moi cong nghe san xuat

Do tien bo ky thuat, hang ngay nhieu loai vat lieu moi duoc phat minh, trong nhung vat lieu moi c6 loai lam thay doi hoan toan cong nghe san xuat vi vay nguoi can bo ky thuat phai nam bat kip thoi de dua trinh do ky thuat luon ? trinh do tién tién. Mat khac may m6c thiet bi cung khong ngung cai tien lam cho nhung cong nghe cu phai doi moi theo,ket qua hinh thanh cong nghe san xuat moi.


## g) Bao vé moi truong ngay cang dugc de cao

Day la khuynh huong phat trien cua thoi dai moi nganh, moi thanh phan xa hoi phai quan tam thich dang. Nhung cong trinh xay dung khong nhung anh huong den moi truong trong thoi gian hoat dong ma no con anh huong lon trong giai doan thi cong. De tranh nhung 6 nhiém do cong truong xay dung gay ra cho dia ban, trong phuong an thi cong phai xem xét den bien phap chong 6 nhiem moi truong.

Nhung 0 nhiem do cong truong gay ra thuong la bui, ban, lam o nhiem bau khong khi va nguon nuoc, chat thai phe liéu xay dung va o nhiem do su gia tang dan so... Tat ca nhung anh huong do phai nhin nhan mot cach nghiém tuc va lap bien phap phong ngua ngay trong cac giai doan thiet ke den khi lap bien phap thi cong.

Nhung bien phap chinh han che o nhiem moi truong do cong truong gay nen co the la:

- Thay doi cong nghé thi cong phu hop.
- Cach ly cong truong voi ben ngoai bang hang rao luoi chan.
- . Lam ve sinh phudng tien cd gioi khi ra khoi cong truong.
- ·C6 ke hoach giai quyet phe thai thuong xuyen, khong de u dong (dong hom ch? di).
- ·Dua mot so bo phan san xuat lan trai c6 the ra xa cong truong, dac biet doi voi cac nguon gay o nhiem (on, bui, phe thai).
- Tang cuong phuong tien van chuyen kin, xe hom, boc bat, tien toi chi su dung vat lieu rdi dong bao, containo (voi, cat, vua kho, da...) vua chong 0 nhiém moi truong vüa chong roi vai, that thoat vat liéu.
- Huong dan trién khai dieu khoan bao vé moi truong trong luat xay dung de moi ngudi dan deu phai thuc hien day du du cong truong lon hay nho. N6 tr? thanh mot tiéeu chi danh gia ho so du thau xay dung.

## i)Ap dung phuong phap to chirc xay dung day chuyen

Tuy dieu kien cu the ta co the ap dung phuong phap t6 chuc tien tien nay sun u sup de o j gu qu uoo uyd oq ao Ky uqd uo cong doan cung mang lai hieu qua nhat dinh vi day la phuong phap t6 chuc san xuat tien tien nhat no bao dam cho su su dung lao dong, tai nguyen hop ly, nang cao chat luong san pham, nang suat lao dong va rut ngan thoi gian thi cong.


## k) Sit dung may tinh dien tit trong quan ly va dieu hanh xay dung

Thoi dai cua cong nghe thong tin cac phan mem su dung trong quan ly n6i chung va xay dung n6i rieng ngay mot nhieu va rat manh, no giup cho nguoi xay dung cap nhat duoc thong tin, du liéu, xu ly tinh huong, nhanh chong mang lai hieu qua cao. Khai thac kha nang may tinh se tao dieu kien cho tu dong hoa phat trien, trong quan ly se mang lai hieu qua kinh te.

## a)Tham doyalap duantienkha thi

La buoc tiep theo cua y tuong duoc chu dau tu thue co quan tu van lam, cung c6 the chu dau tu truc tiep thuc hien. Noi dung cua buoc nay la tham do cac so lieu ban dau de chu dau tu khang dinh y tuong d6 c6 co s? khong, neu co trien vong tiep tuc nghién cuu tiep bang khong thi dung lai.

![Page 9](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/cc0c8e04-35fc-41d9-ae3b-c13ea6f26d6d.png)

---

Trong buoc nay cong tac tham do la chu yeu, dua trén nhung so lieu san c6 thu thap duoc, nguoi ta lam du an tién kha thi. Sau d6 lam nhung bai toan chu yeu la phan tich kinh te so bo de ket luan.

Lap du an tien kha thi can lam nhung viec sau:

- .Tim hieu nhu cau cua xa hoi trong khu vuc du an hoat dong.
- . Tim hieu chu truong duong loi phat trien kinh te cua quoc gia trong thoi gian kha dai (10 - 50 nam).
- a r an, trong d6 chu trong den trinh do cong nghe, nang suat hien co va kha nang phat trien cua cac co s? hien dien trong thoi gian toi (cai tao, m? rong, nang cap cong nghe, hien dai hoa cong nghe).
- Trinh do cong nghe san xuat cua khu vuc va the gioi.
- Muc song cua xa hoi, kha nang tieu thu san pham tai dia phuong va khu vuc xuat khau.
- Kha nang cua ch? dau tu, cac nguon von co the huy dong, mo hinh dau tu.
- Nguon cung cap nguyen vat lieu, cong nghe san xuat.
- Dia ban xay dung cong trinh se trien khai du an voi so lieu ve dia hinh, khi hau, dan cu, moi truong truoc va sau khi xay dung cong trinh.
- .Co s? ha tang san co va trien vong trong tuong lai.

## b) Lap du án kha thi

Day la buoc quan trong trong qua trinh hinh thanh du an, n6 khang dinh tinh hién thuc cua du an (kha thi). Trong buoc nay gom co hai phan khao sat va viet bao cao kinh te ky thuat. Lap du an kha thi thuong duoc cd np qu su rud n uy ue p u 'uty onua gy in ua n unb tinh kha thi ky thuat va tinh hieu qua kinh te cua cong trinh (sua chua, m? rong, hién dai hoa, xay moi).

Cong trinh cang lon, cang phuc tap, dia ban xay dung cang rong thi viéc khao sat cang phai toan dien va day du. Doi voi nhung khu vuc da co cong trinh xay dung thi so lieu co the tan dung nhung ket qua cua lan khao sat truoc.

![Page 10](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/e55630c5-9840-45c0-a4d1-cac3ef171354.png)

---

Trong khao sat chia ra lam hai loai kinh te va ky thuat. Khao sat ve kinh te thuong dudc thuc hien truoc, no cung cap so lieu lam co s? xac dinh vi tri cung voi nguon nguyén liéu, mang luoi ky thuat ha tang co s? (giao thong, nang luong) nguon nuoc, dan cu, phong tuc, van hoa, moi truong thién nhien, nhan luc v.v..

Doi tuong cua khao sat ky thuat la dieu kién thien nhien trong khu vuc trien khai du an, muc dich de trien khai du an co lgi nhat. Ket qua khao Suo 'eno equ yoeoy Anb gunp Aex gugq zeu uoyo eni dnia aenya fy 49s trinh, nhung giai phap ky thuat can trien khai.

Ket luan cuoi cung cua du an dua tren su danh gia toan dien kinh te - ky thuat cac phuong an dat ra.

Doi voi cong trinh dan dung va cong nghiep khao sat bao gom nhung van de.

- . Lam ro dieu kien kinh te khu vuc xay dung voi su quan tam cho hoat dien, nuoc, mang luoi giao thong, lao dong cung nhu cac tai nguyen khac, nhung khao sat giup viec xac dinh vi tri xay dung cong trinh.
- Khao sat nhung cong trinh dang hoat dong trong khu vuc se xay dung cong trinh, lam ro cong suat, trinh do cong nghe, kha nang lien ket giua chung va voi cong trinh se xay. Day la cd s? de xac dinh quy mo va loi ich cua cong trinh se xay dung.
- Khao sat toan dien dia hinh, dia vat khu vuc trien khai du an de thiet ke va quy hoach cac nha, cong trinh cung nhu cac mang luoi ky thuat, s o yuy e gp uq ua ury o oonp Aeu oi uo os p aua y hoac phai tu do ve).
- Khao sat dia chat cong trinh, dia chat thuy van cua mat bang xay dung de xac dinh tinh co ly cua dia tang, nuoc ngam, mua gi6, h6 ao, song suoi...S6 lieu khao sat phai du de xac dinh duoc giai phap ket cau, mong, he thong mang luoi nuoc ngam...
- Khao sat dieu kien thoi tiet khu vuc xay dung (mua nang, nhiet do, sam o o a s (

- nhung yeu to cua khi quyen (do am, do trong sach cua khong khi,phong xa, ion...).
- Khao sat dieu kien lien quan den xay dung de van dung kha nang tai cho giam gia thanh cong trinh. Trong khao sat nay bao gom nguon vat liéu xay dung tai ch6 (may moc, thiet bi, giao thong, kha nang khai thac cac xi nghiep phu tro) nguon nhan cong dia phuong; mang luoi dien, nuoc san co.
- Khao sat nhung yeu t6 anh huong den gia thanh cong trinh, thoi han co the hoan thanh xay dung tung phan va toan bo cung nhu ke hoach dua cong trinh vao khai thac.
- Khao sat quang canh kien truc, quy hoach khu vuc de cong trinh co giai phap thiet ke hoa nhap voi canh quan kien truc san co.

Tat ca cac so hieu c6 lien quan den thiet ke, xay dung va khai thac cong trinh deu phai thu thap day du va viet thanh bao cao kinh te ky thuat de khang dinh vi tri xay dung cong trinh. Bao cao phai dua ra it nhat la hai phuong an de so sanh lua chon.

Bao cao kinh.te ky thuat cua du an kha thi duoc thuc hien b?i co quan tu van thiet ke dua tren nhung bao cao khao sat kinh te - ky thuat. Bao cao phai dua ra loi giai cua bai toan dat ra it nhat c6 hai phuong an. Trong d6 chung minh tinh hieu qua kinh te cua loi giai bao gom nhung phan chinh sau:

- 1) Cong suat cua cong trinh
- 2) Gia tri, hieu qua kinh te, thdi han thu h6i von dau tu co ban cua cong trinh.
- 3) Thoi gian dat cong suat thiet ke va cac chi tieu kinh te ky thuat thiet ke.
- 4) Muc do co gioi hoa, tu dong hoa cac qua trinh san xuat, trinh do cong nghe so voi trong nuoc va the gioi. Trinh do tiéu chuan hoa, thong nhat hoa cac chi tiet trong san pham lam ra. He thong quan ly chat luong ap dung.
- 5) Hé so xay dung (su dung mat bang) so voi tieu chuan quy dinh.
- 6) Su thay d6i moi truong sinh thai (cay coi, dong chay, gia dat dai) do
7. cong trinh mang lai.

![Page 11](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/8a2a9235-3a53-4b40-b9a2-f00e91acc87c.png)

---

- 7) Anh huong den doi song xa hoi cua nhan vien va gia dinh cong nhan, can bo trong qua trinh xay dung va khai thac cong trinh.

Ho so cua bao cao du an kha thi bao gom:

- 1) Thuyet minh trinh bay tom tat noi dung cac phuong an dua ra de lua chon, so sanh cac phuong an do, tinh toan khai quat nhung quyet dinh trong phuong an, trinh bay bien phap an toan lao dong va tinh toan cac chi tiéu kinh te ky thuat, trong do giai thich va cach xac dinh cac chi tieu do.
- 2) Tong mat bang the hien day du moi lien he giua cac toa nha, cac cong trinh xay dung.
- 3) Cac ban ve cong nghe, giao thong noi bo, giai phap kién truc, ket cau, he thong thiet bi va cac giai phap thiet ke khac co lien quan.
- 4) Danh muc cac loai may moc, thiet bi cua cac hang muc cong trinh.
- 5) Uoc tinh muc dau tu xay dung cong trinh (khai toan).
- 6) Uoc tinh gia mua sam thiet bi, may moc theo gia khao sat.
- 7) Tong muc dau tu cuia du an (tong khai toan).
- 8) Bang thong ke cac loai cong tac xay lap chinh.
- 9) Phan loai vat liéu noi, ngoai de lap ke hoach tiep nhan.
- 10) Phan loai theo nghe nghiep lao dong can thiet.
- 11) Thiet ke t6 chuc (hoac thi cong) xay dung voi tong tien do (the hien bang bieu do ngang hoac mang).
- 12) Dung ma két cong trinh (toan bo hoac phan chinh) neu thay can thiet.
- 13) Phu luc huong dan tuyen dung lao dong (theo luat lao dong) va khai thac nguon kinh phi xay dung cong trinh (dac biet la ngoai té).
- 14) Cac giai phap ky thuat chong 0 nhiém moi truong hay thay d6i canh quan.

## 1.5. THIET KE CONG TRiNH XAY DUNG DAN DUNG VA CONG NGHIEP

ud 1ey wog oeq dnqau huo ba gunp uep unp Kex qu guoo ay aanu chinh la thiet ke'va tinh du toan.

Tuy theo quy mo, do phuc tap tinh quan trong cua cong trinh thiet ke c6 the thuc hien theo hai giai doan (cong trinh nhom A va nhung cong trinh quan trong cua nhom B) hay thiet ke mot giai doan. Moi thiet ke bieu hien mot giai doan hoan chinh thiet ke voi chat luong va do chinh xac nhat dinh. Thiet ke giai doan sau dua trén ket qua cua giai doan truoc de hoan thien va cu the hoa cac giai phap lua chon.

Neu thiet ke mot giai doan la thiet ke thi cong. Khi thiet ke hai giai doan thi giai doan dau la thiet ke ky thuat con giai doan sau la thiet ke thi cong a a 4y up doy on ut ua 4a unb p op a ht ('1 u) op huo unb ayo qu 6a ou Anb An uop 1a jey Aey hou gy ht ' np co quan co chu quyen quyét dinh.

Nhiem vu chinh cua cd quan thiet ke la khong ngüng nang cao chat luong cua du an, giam gia thanh cong trinh, rut ngan thoi gian tang nang xuat cong tac thiet ke.

![Page 12](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/a4294cd2-106e-4f31-b485-67b5bc05a50c.png)

---

## A. Nguyen tac thiet ke cong trinh xay dung

De dat duoc muc dich tren khi thiet ke can thoa man nhung yeu cau sau:

- 1) Thiet ke dong bo cong trinh xay dung.

Nghia la song song voi thiét ke cong nghe can tien hanh thiet ke kien truc, ket cau xay dung cong trinh. Nhu vay se bao dam su an khop giua cac phan thiet ke de co the dat mua sam thiet bi may moc kip thoi. De dam bao tinh kha thi khi thiet ke da phai hinh thanh cac bien phap xay dung cong trinh (thu tu, phuong tién, thoi gian thi cong). Vi muc dich d6 trong thiet ke phai co thiet ke to chuc (hoac thi cong) xay dung di kem voi thiet ke cong nghe, kien truc va ket cau de dam bao tinh kha thi cua no.

- 2) Hieu qua kinh te va hoan thien ky thuat trong cac giai phap thiet ke ph?i phu hop voi quy hoach, tuan thu cac quy dinh trong quy chuan xay dung cua nha nuoc Viet Nam (TCVN) hay nhung tiéu chuan da dugc quy dinh trong hop dong thiet ke.
- 3) Ap dung rong rai nhung thiet ke mau co chat luong; nhung chi tiet ket cau ph6 bien trong cong trinh. Day la bien phap giam chi phi thiet ke, nang cao cong nghiep h6a, rut ngan thoi gian xay dung cong trinh.

- 4) Su dung toi da vat lieu dia phuong giam toi muc thap nhat vat liéu nhap va hiem.
- 5) Ap dung cong nghe tien tien trong cong tac xay lap lam co s? co gioi hoa dong bo, van dung hinh thuc t6 chuc va quan ly xay dung tien tien.
- 6) Tien hanh khao sat b6 sung doi v6i nhung giai phap ky thuat ma nhung khao sat giai doan du an cung cap chua day du, hoac phat hien, phat sinh nhung so lieu moi.


## B. Thiét ké ky thuat (TKKT)

Thiet ke ky thuat la giai doan dau trong thiet ke hai giai doan. Thiet ke ky thuat dua trén co s? khao sat va bao cao du an kha thi cong voi khao sat trong giai doan thiet ke (neu co).

(de b6 sung) chua du chi tiet de tien hanh thi cong, dac biet la cac chi tiet do thi cong dat ra. Thiet ke ky thuat duoc trien khai ? tat ca cac phan cua thiet ke (cong nghe, kien truc, ket cau, vat lieu v.y..), san sang de trien khai thiet ke thi cong.

![Page 13](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/ff13e5b1-6d0f-4912-b2ca-254269e70051.png)

---

H6 so thiet ke ky thuat bao gom:

- 1) Thuyet minh trinh bay cach tinh toan, khai quat nhung giai phap thiet ke cua toan bo cong trinh.
- 2) Cac ban ve cong nghe, day chuyen san xuat, giai phap kien truc (mat bang, cat, dung) giai phap ket cau, giai phap trang thiet bi..v..v. ma
3. cong nghe.thi cong se ap dung.
- 3) Du toan so bo gia thanh cong trinh.

## C. Thiet ke thi cong (TKTC)

Thiet ke thi cong la thiet ke cong trinh theo mot giai doan hay giai doan hai cua thiet ke cong trinh hai giai doan gom thiet ke va du toan.

Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS http://www.vietcons.org ky thuat (thiet ke hai giai doan). Nhung sai lech voi du an kha thi hay thiet ke ky thuat chi duoc phep khi nang cao chat luong thiet ke va duoc chu dau tu va nguoi thiet ke truoc dong y.

Khi thiet ke mot giai doan TKTC phai giai quyet toan bo va dut diem nhung giai phap thiet ke, cung cap du so lieu can thiet nhu lao dong, tai nguyen, vat tu, ky thuat, gia thanh xay dung (du toan) cung voi day du cac ban ve thi cong cac cong tac xay lap cho nguoi xay dung.

Trong thiet ke hai giai doan TKTC phai cu the hoa, chi tiet hoa cac giai phap cong nghe. kien truc, ket cau. thi cong da duoc khang dinh trong thiet ke ky thuat.

don gia ap dung, chi dan hoac thong tu cua cd quan nha nuoc ve hach toan uo uo p i e up unb u n n np 9o rud uo i qu uoo trinh (giay uu tien, quyet dinh mien giam thue...).

![Page 14](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/fde53b88-1aef-454a-b140-d6280983f1b2.png)

---

Ban ve thiet ke thi cong phai cung cap day du chinh xac, ro rang cac chi tiet can thiet cua cong trinh thiet ke de tien hanh cac cong tac xay lap. Thiet ke phai trinh bay tu tong the den chi tiet, phan tich chi dan (gom mat bang, cat, chi tiet phong to, trién khai bo phan). Ban ve phai chi duoc vi tri, moi quan hé giua cac cong trinh, toa nha voi mang luoi ky thuat, giao thong. Ban vé chi s g r o ho r p n o nu p o d o n a chinh xac don gian nhat. tiet kiém nhat.

Du toan thiet ke duoc tinh toan dua tren khang dinh cua du an kha thi va da trien khai day du thiet ke thi cong (ke ca nhung bién phap thi cong dac biet chua co trong dinh muc don gia den thoi diem tinh toan).

Thiet ke thi cong va du toan kem theo phai duoc cap quan ly co tham quyen phe duyet n6 lam tai lieu phuc vu thi cong vua la co s? de quyet toan cong trinh.


## 1.6. THIEt Ke tO cHUc xAy DUNG


## A. Nhiem vy va nguyen tac thiet ke t6 chuc, thi cong xay dung

Trong thiet ke cong trinh xay dung luon luon phai bao gom thiet ke t6 chuc h 1 n o o u oa o Ax oo tn oo thuat va thiet ke thi cong.

Nhiem vu cua thiet ket6 chucva thi cong la timkiembien phap t6 chuchop ly dexay dung cong trinh trong thoi han ngan nhat c6 the,voi gia thanh nh? nhat,chat luong totnhat theoyeu cau thiet ke.

Trong thiet ke to chuc va thi congxay dung phai trinh bay phuong phap, phuong tien,thoi han thuchien tungloai cong tacxaylap cungnhu toanbo cong trinh.Do la cd s delap kehoach dau tu von,cung ungvat tu ky thuat, xac dinh lan trai, co so san xuat phu tro. N6 la cd so de lap ke hoach thuc hien kiem tra,bao cao san xuat.

De thiet ke t chuc va thi cong xay dung dat duoc nhiem vu dera (nhanh, chatluong,gia ha) khi thietkephai tuan theo cacnguyen tac sau:

![Page 15](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/62ce4dd7-ec30-428b-8204-845d9c9c6dd2.png)

---

- 1)Viec thuchiencac cong tacxay lapbatbuocphai tuan theo(quy trinh quy pham) da duoc phe chuan delam chinh xac,ket hop nhip nhang,an khop giua cac qua trinh san xuat va giua cac don vi tham gia xay dung.
- 2) Dua phuong phap san xuat day chuyen vao to chuc thuc hien cang nhieu cang tot.Day la phuong phap tien tien no se nang cao nang suat lao dong, chat ludng cong viec; san xuat dieu hoa, lien tuc, giam nhe cong tac chi dao va kiem tra chat ludng, de dang ap dung cac phuong phap quan ly he thong (vi du ISO.9000).
- 3) Dua he thong quan ly chat luong lien ticn vao trong san xuat (thay KCS bang ISO - 900o) de nang tinh canh tranh trong co che thi truong.
- 4） Baodam sanxuatquanh nam,nhuvay sekhai thachetnangluc thiet bi,bao dam cong anviec lam cho can bo cong nhan,tao su phat trien on dinh cho don vi xay lap trong thoi gian dai.
- 5)Su dungcdgioihoa dongbova tu donghoa trongcacqua trinhxaylap. Chon nhung may moc. cd gioi co cong suat manh va gia thanh ha,su dung het cong xuat va he so thoi gian cao.
- 6)Su dungcacket cau lapghep va cau kien sanxuat tainha mayderut ngan thoi gian thi cong giam phu phi (cop pha,hao hut vat lieu..).
- 7 Giam khoi luong xay dung lan trai, nha tam.
- Tang cuong si dung nhung loai nha thao lap,di dong, su dung nhieu lan vao muc dich tam tren cong truong de giam gia thanh cong trinh.
- 8)Baodam dieu kien doi songvanhoa tinh thanva dieukienmoi truong sinh hoat cho cong nhan cung nhu dan cu quanh vung cong truong.

Thuc hien phap lénh phong ho lao dong,ky thuat an toan lao dong cung nhu phap lenh phong chong chay no tai cong truong.

- 9)Ap dung cac dinh muc tien tien trong lap kehoach,t6 chuc,chi dao xay dung voi viec su dung sd do mang va may tinh (su dung cac phan mem lapkehoach va quanly trongxaydung).
- cho can bo cong nhan di doivoi ap dung he thongquan ly chat luong de tang tinh chu dong,trach nhiem cua cac ca nhan cung nhu tap thevoi cong viec.
- 11) Bao dam thoi han xay dung cong trinh theo phap lenh (hop dong ky ket).
- 12) Luontiep canva thu thapkinh nghiemquan ly tien tien trongnganh va thegioi ap dungvao cong viec xay lap cungnhu tochuc denang cao cac chi sokinh te ky thuat,doi moi doanh nghiep xay dunghoa nhap voi khu vuc.


## B.Thiet ke t6 chuc xay dumg (TKTCXD)

thiet ke thi cong trong thietkemot giai doan.Thiet ke to chucxay dung duoc cdquan tuvanthietkethuchien trencdsabaocaoduankha thivaketqua khaosatkythuatvakhaosatbosungtrongkhi thietke.

Nhitngnhiem uu can giai quyet trong TKTCXD:

- 1) Xac dinh thoi han xay dung cong trinh cung nhu cac giai doan chinh, thoi diem tiep nhan thietbi.
- 2) Nhung giai phap cd ban ve to chuc xay dung toan cong truong cung nhu tingnha mot.
- 3) Xac dinh khoi luong dau tu tien von cho tung nam, quy.
- 4) Thanh phan,khoi luong, thu tu, thdi han thuc hien cac cong viec trong giai doan chuan bi kh?i cong cong truong.
- 5) Nhu cau ve tai nguyen vat chat ky thuat chinh,nguon cung cap va cach thuc tiep nhan trong tiung giai doan cu the.

- 6) Nhu cau sinh hoat cua cong nhan, can bo ky thuat, nhu cau nha cüa, sinh hoat cua cong truong va giai phap ap dung.
- 7) Xay dung co so san xuat phu trd phuc vu cong truong xay dung.
- 8) Trien khai phieu cong nghe cho nhung cong viec thuc hien cong nghe moi.
- 9) Thiet lap dieu kien an toan lao dong va vé sinh moi truong cho toan cong truong va cho tung cong viec dac biet.


## Ho so cua TKTCXD bao gom nhitng tai lieu sau:

![Page 16](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/5eec7fb7-5bf8-401b-bb8f-7bf78295e1c6.png)

---

- p h 0 5 d x a 5o dy n ( xay dung chinh cung nhu trong giai doan chuan bi.
- 2) Tong tien do (duoi dang khai quat) thuc hién cac cong viéc chinh trong giai doan chuan bi va giai doan xay dung. Trong d6 the hien ro thu tu trien khai cong viec cac giai doan hoan thanh va toan bo. Trong tien d? co kem theo bieu do huy dong nhan luc, thoi gian su dung may moc.
- 3) Ké hoach tong the ve cung cap von theo nam, quy phu hop voi tung tien do.
- 4) Tong mat bang xay dung voi ty le thich hop the hien nhung cong trinh da va se xay dung cung nhu kho tang lan trai.
- 5) Ban d6 khu vuc voi ty le thich hop the hien cong truong xay dung cung voi mang luoi xi nghiép phu trd, co s? vat chat ky thuat, lang cong nhan, he thong giao thong duong xa va nhung cong trinh khac co lien quan den cong truong.
- 6) Danh muc tong the nhung vat lieu, ban san pham, ket cau chinh, may xay dung, phuong tien van tai theo cac giai doan xay dung cua nam, quy.
- 7) Thiet ke va du toan nha ? lan trai tam khong nam trong gia thanh xay dung. Nhung cong trinh lan trai, phuc vu thi cong nay do cap quyet dinh dau tu xem xet va phe duyét.
- 8) Ban thuyet minh trinh bay dac diem cong trinh, dieu kien thi cong, quy trinh cong nghe, phudng phap to chuc xay dung, nhu cau va giai phap ve nhan tai vat luc, may moc, kho bai, duong xa, lan trai cung nhu cac chi so ve kinh te ky thuat cua bien phap thi cong.

- 9) Tinh gia du toan cong trinh dua trén nhung don gia, dinh muc va bién phap thi cong dudc phe duyet. Tinh du toan phai co bang ke cong viéc, cach xac dinh khoi luong cong viec cua chung. Doi voi nhieu viéc khong co don gia thi phai co tinh toan gia thanh bao gom vat liéu, nhan cong, may moc.

Thiet ke to chuc xay dung phai duoc tien hanh song song voi cac giai doan thiet ke tuong ung cua cong trinh de phu hop voi cac giai phap mat bang, ket cau, cong nghe cua cong trinh.

Doi voi nhung cong trinh don vi nh? gon hoac cong trinh thi cong theo thiet ke mau phai co:

- . Tong tien do (dang mang)
- .Bieu do nhan luc va su dung may moc
- ·Bang tinh du toan
- . Thuyet minh.


## C. Thiet ke t6 chuc thi cong (TKTCTC)

o os po ua uay onu dyi Aex unb oo oonp uoo tun onuo gn ay aa TKTCXD, du toan cong trinh cong voi nhung ket qua, khao sat b6 sung khu cong truong va nang luc cua don vi nhan thau.

quyet cac van de moi phat hien. Dac biet quan tam den nhung chi tiet trien vu san xuat vat lieu, cau kien cho cong truong. Vi tri, cong suat, cong nghe va p Aeya ns 'ai oyn uaanau ni oonp yd datyau yx seo eno iq gu chi dugc phep khi ben thiet ke TCXD dong y do giam duoc gia thanh cong trinh va cai thien chat luong cong viéc.

TKTCTC phuc vu cho cong tac t6 chuc thuc hién, chi dao va kiem tra tat ca cac giai doan thi cong, cac hang muc cong trinh va toan cong truong nen thiet o sup kex uy toun :ns an ua oo oex quo ea gun no ons aay yd ay hang muc cong trinh, cua cac giai doan chinh va toan cong truong; thu tu va bién phap thuc hien cac cong viéc xay lap; su phoi hop, thoi han thuc hién cac bien phap trong giai doan chuan bi; bieu d6 cung ung vat tu, may moc; nhu cau ve nhien lieu nang luong trong cac giai doan thi cong; nhu cau ve nhan luc theo nganh nghe; bién phap phong ho, vé sinh an toan lao dong; he thong kiem tra, quan ly chat luong ap dung.

![Page 17](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/5a0268e9-4af8-4736-9f9b-660eb93f4b79.png)

---

## Ho so cua TKTCTC bao gom:

- 1) Tien do (dang SDM) xay dung cac cong trinh don.vi voi khoi luong thi cong chinh xac.
- 2) Tong tién do (dang SDM) khai quat cho toan cong truong va cac giai doan xay dung.
- 3) Tong mat bang bo tri chinh xac vi tri cac xi nghiep san xuat, duong xa c6 dinh va tam, kho, bai mang luoi cap dien, nuoc thong tin...
- 4) Ban liet ke khoi luong cac cong viec trong giai doan chuan bi va bieu do thuc hien.
5. 5)Bieu do cung ung vat tu chinh.
- 6) Bieu d6 nhu cau nhan luc theo nganh nghe, may xay dung va van chuyen.
- 7) Phieu cong nghe cho nhung cong viéc thi cong phuc tap va moi.
- 8) H6 so may m6c va phieu chuyen giao cong nghe cho nhung cong viéc thi cong dac biet, quan trong (n6 min, khoan, ke...)
- 9) Ban thuyet minh ve cac giai phap cong nghe, bao hiem, moi truong, an toan lao dong, hinh thuc tiep nhan nhan tai, vat luc. Tinh toan cac chi tieu kinh te ky thuat (thdi han xay dung, trinh do co gioi hoa, chi phi lao dong cho mot don vi san pham..)
- 10) Cac ban ve thiet ke thi cong cong trinh tam, lan trai. TKTCTC phai thuc hien xong truoc ngay kh?i cong cong trinh mot thoi gian (khoang 2 thang) de can bo ky thuat nghien cuu lam quen va nam bat duoc y d6. Viec thiet ke TKTCTC phai duoc ky su va can bo ky thuat co kinh nghiém cua don vi nhan thau thuc hien duoi su chi dao cua ky su truong co su tham khao y kien cua nhung ngudi thi cong.

TKTCTC giai quyet cac van de ky thuat cong nghe, t6 chuc va kinh te phuc ni os po wei ue suonyd natyu yuey uo 1yd yun nn ton oonp hep uon de chon theo nhung chi tiéu kinh te ky thuat, cu the la:


## Ve ky thuat

- Bao dam chat luong cao nhat
- ·Tao dieu kien cho viec thi cong de dang, an toan nhat.


## Vekinh te'

- Giam gia thanh thap nhat.
- Su dung co hieu qua nhat tai nguyen san xuat cua don vi xay lap.
- Dua cong trinh vao khai thac dung ke hoach.

## LAP TIEN DO TRONG SAN XUAT XAY DUNG

![Page 18](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/0b72761e-645f-419f-9515-08b1689d0688.png)

## 2. 1. NHIEM VU VA NOI DUNG CUA LAP kE HOACH LiCH (tIEN DO)

Xay dung dan dung va cong nghiep cung nhu cac nghanh san xuat khac muon dat dugc nhung muc dich de ra phai co mot ke hoach.san xuat cu the. Mot ke hoach san xuat duoc gan lien voi mot truc thoi gian nguoi ta goi d6 la ke hoach lich hay tien do. Nhu vay tien do la mot ke hoach duoc gan lien voi nien lich. Moi thanh phan cua tien do duoc gan tren mot truc thoi gian xac dinh.

Cong truong xay dung duoc tien hanh b?i nhieu t6 chuc xay lap voi su tham gia cua nha thau, nguoi thiet ke, doanh nghiép cung ung may moc thiet bi va cac loai tai nguyen... Nhu vay xay dung mot cong trinh la mot he dieu khien phuc tap, rong lon. Vi trong he c6 rat nhieu thanh phan va moi quan hé giua chung rat phuc tap. Su phuc tap ca ve so luong cac thanh phan va trang thai cua no la bien dong va ngau nhien. Vi vay trong xay dung cong trinh khong the dieu khien chinh xac ma co tinh xac suat. De xay dung mot cong trinh phai co mot mo hinh khoa hoc dieu khien cac qua trinh - t6 chuc va chi dao viéc xay dung. Mo hinh d6 chinh la tien do thi cong.

Khi xay dung mot cong trinh phai thuc hien rat nhieu cac qua trinh xay lap lien quan chat che voi nhau trong mot khong gian va thoi gian xac dinh voi tai nguyen co gioi han. Nhu vay muc dich cua lap tien do la thanh lap mot mo hinh san suat, trong d6 sap xep cac cong viec sao cho bao dam xay dung cong trinh trong thoi gian ngan, gia thanh ha, chat luong cao.

Muc dich nay co the cu the nhu sau:

- 1) Ket thuc va dua cac hang muc cong trinh tung phan cung nhu tong the vao hoat dong dung thdi han dinh truoc.
- 2) Su dung hop ly may moc thiet bi.

- 3) Giam thieu thoi gian u dong tai nguyen chua su dung.
- 4) Lap ke hoach su dung toi uu ve co s? vat chat ky thuat phuc vu xay dung.
- 5) Cung cap kip thoi cac giai phap co hieu qua de tien hanh thi cong cong trinh.

Tien do luon duoc bieu hien duoi dang bieu do.Tuy theo tinh chat cua cac cong trinh va yeu cau cua cong nghe, hinh thuc the hien bieu do co the bieu dien duoi dang ngang, xien hay mang (se trinh bay ? cac muc tiep theo).

Tom lai, tien do la ke hoach san xuat the hien bang bieu d6 noi dung bao gom cac so lieu tinh toan, cac giai phap duoc ap dung trong thi cong bao gom: cong nghe. thoi gian, dia diem, vi tri va khoi luong cac cong viec xay lap cung voi dieu kien thuc hien chung.

![Page 19](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/d1a5f503-0902-4458-b9f3-c7dd5553c037.png)

---

Tien do la bo phan khong the tach roi cua thiet ke to chuc xay dung va thiet ke to chuc thi cong.

Tien do trong thiet ke ve to chuc xay dung goi tat la tien do to chuc xay dung (TDTCxD) do co quan tu van thiet ke lap bao gom ke hoach thuc hien cac cong viec: thiet ke, chuan bi, thi cong, hop dong cung cap may moc, thiet bi, cung cap ho so tai lieu phuc vu thi cong va dua cong trinh vao hoat dong. Bieu do tien do neu la cong trinh nh? the hien bang duong ngang, neu la lon phuc tap the hien bang mang. Trong tien do cac cong viec the hien duoi dang tong quat, nhieu cong viec cua cong trinh don vi duoc nhom lai the hien bang mot cong viec tong hop. Trong tong tien do phai chi ra duoc nhung thoi diem chu chot nhu giai doan xay dung, ngay hoan thanh cua cac hang muc xay dung, thoi diem cung cap may moc, thiet bi cho cong trinh va ngay hoan thanh toan bo.

Tien do trong thiet ke t6 chuc thi cong xay dung goi tat la tien do thi cong

(TDTC) do don vi nhan thau (B) lap voi su tham gia cua cac nha thau phu (B'). Trong d6 the hien cac cong viec chuan bi, xay dung tam, xay dung chinh va thoi gian dua tung hang muc cong trinh vao hoat dong. Tien do thi cong co the the hien bang duong ngang hay mang. Tong tien do lap dua vao tien do cac cong trinh don vi. Cac cong trinh don vi khi lien ket voi nhau dua tren su ket hop cong nghe va su dung tai nguyen. Trong tien do don vi cac cong viec xay lap duoc xac dinh chi tiet tung chung loai, khoi luong theo tinh toan cua thiet ke thi cong. Thoi han hoan thanh cac hang muc cong trinh va toan bo cong truong phai dung voi tien do to chuc xay dung.

Tien do dung de chi dao thi cong xay dung, de danh gia su sai léch giua thuc 0 aupnn guoo Anu yo oq ueo ionsu dnia dei 5p yoeoy gy ea nenx ues 9 nhung quyet dinh de dieu chinh thi cong. Neu su sai lech giua san xuat va ke n de p u ti dn ud op ou o up ui nb p u) oy 'nep ueq nan onu opnp nie oqo oes op wgip youn yea suen onya uaan enp iou neu sai lech cang it cang tot, nhat la thoi han xay dung.


## 2.2. CAC BUOC LAP TIEN DO

Tien do thi cong duoc lap dua tren so lieu va tinh toan cua thiet ke t6 chuc xay dung hoac thiet ke t6 chuc thi cong cung voi nhung ket qua khao sat b6 sung do dac diem cua cong truong. Trong nhung so lieu d6, dac biet quan tam den thoi han cua cac hang muc cong trinh va toan bo cong truong.

De tien do lap chong thoa man nhiem vu de ra va hop ly, nguoi can bo ky thuat (chuyen gia lap tien do) phai tien hanh cac buoc nhu sau (xem hinh 2.1):

- 1) Phan tich cong nghe xay dung cong trinh.
- 2) Lap bieu danh muc cong viec se tien hanh xay lap cong trinh.
- 3) Xac dinh khoi luong cong viec theo danh muc trong bieu.
- 4) Chon bien phap ky thuat thi cong cho cac cong viéc.
5. ony (e e) oou Au ea (auoo 6u) auop oi yyd yyo qup ox (9 cong viec.
- 6) Xac dinh thoi gian thi cong va chi phi tai nguyén.
- 7) Lap tien do ban dau.
8. 8)Xac dinh chi tieu kinh te KT.
- 9) So sanh cac chi tieu cua tien do vüa lap voi chi tiéu de ra (muc dich).
- 10) Toi uu tien do theo cac chi so uu tien.
- 11) Tien do chap nhan.
- 12) Lap bieu nhu cau tai nguyen.

## 1. Phan tich cong nghe

La buoc kh?i dau nhung vo cung quan trong. N6 dinh huong cho cac giai aunp Kex au suoo oonp yon uud uon nes ga uoo as duau suoo deyd phai dua tren thiet ke cong nghe, kien truc va ket cau cua cong trinh. Phan tich kha nang thi cong cong trinh tren quan diem chon cong nghe thuc hien cac qua trinh xay lap hop ly va su can thiet may moc va vat lieu phuc vu thi cong.

Viec phan tich cong nghe san xuat bat dau ngay khi thiet ke cong trinh do unp Aex uaty onun lonau ioa oeq ueq ns 9 1n nuo gy aaun uga n unb 0o doi khi phai c6 y kien cua cac ben lién quan khac nhu nguoi cap von, don quip taanb gunyu gp uery ngip oe as np Aep oea doy ns auoo yyn na onyd dung vat lieu dia phuong hay nhap ngoai, giai phap mong, ket cau chiu luc, trang bi noi that). Sau khi da chon cong nghe thi cong ta tien hanh phan tich cong nghe d6.

Phan tich cong nghe xay lap dé lap tien do thi cong do cd quan xay dung tich dac diem san suat de nam vung cong nghe xay lap lam cho cac don vi thuc hien hieu tuong tan cong viec va se co bien phap ky thuat thich hop. Nhung bién phap ap dung chi duoc phép hoan thién them cong nghe da trinh... quan trong hon tat ca la khong lam kéo dai thoi gian thi cong.


## 2. Lap danh muc cong viec xay lap

Dua vao su phan tich cong nghe san suat va nhung tinh toan trong thiet viéc phan chia cac qua trinh thanh nhung cong viéc phai th?a man nhung dieu kien sau:

![Page 21](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/e3023695-3bf8-41e7-9281-2336822db4a2.png)

---

- . Tén cong viec trung voi ma so trong dinh muc su dung.
- Cong viec co the tien hanh thi cong doc lap ve khong gian cung nhu thoi gian khong bi va cung khong gay can tr? cho nhung cong viec khac.
- .Mot cong viec phai du khoi luong cho mot don vi (t6, doi) lam viec trong thoi gian nhat dinh.

- Trong kha nang co the nen phan chia moi viec cho mot don vi chuyen n gq yo gun uo doy uon o u 'qu p boy uo nang hay hon hop thuc hien.
- Tai thoi diem ket thuc cac giai doan xay dung cong trinh cac cong viéc lien quan cung ket thuc tai thoi diem d6.
- Nhung cong viec khong thuc hien tai hien truong nhung nam trong quy trinh xay lap cung phai dua vao danh muc (vi du tiep nhan may moc, khuyech dai cau kien, van chuyen xe may...).
- Nhung cong viec lon co the chia ra thanh nhieu cong viec nho de co the ket hop thi cong song song voi cac cong viec khac de rut ngan thoi gian thi cong.
- Nhieu cong viec nho, khoi luong it co the gop lai thanh mot cong viéc duoi mot ten chung de don gian khi the hien bieu do.


## 3. Xac dinh khoi luong cong viéc

Khoi luong cong viec luon di kem voi ban danh muc duoc tinh toan xac dinh theo ban ve thi cong va thuyet minh cua thiet ke. Don vi cua khoi luong thuong dung la cac don vi do luong (m, m?, m?, t , cai, chiec..) cung co the dung don vi tien te tuong duong trong dinh muc, don gia su dung. Xac dinh dung khoi luong la cd s? chon phuong tién, phuong an thi cong hop ly. Tu d6 xac dinh chinh xac nhan luc, may moc va thoi gian thi cong de lap tien do.


## 4. Chon bien phap ky thuat thi cong

Tren co s? khoi luong cong viec va dieu kien lam viec ta chon bien phap thi cong. Trong chon bien phap thi cong uu tién su dung cd gioi se rut ngan thoi gian thi cong cung tang nang suat lao dong giam gia thanh. Chon may d no u 'q aup bo p, on nb oa un u oo may cho cong viéc chu dao hay cong viéc co khoi luong lon truoc sau d6 chon cac may con lai. Truong hop co nhieu phuong an kha thi trong chon sup ns enn 4y 9 quy n go oo qus os yuy un rud so A bien phap thi cong thu cong chi trong truong hop dieu kien thi cong khong

Khi chon may ngoai tinh nang ky thuat phai phu hop ta can chu y dén nang suat va su anh huong cua bien phap thi cong den moi truong xung quanh (ban, bui, on.).


## 5. Chon cac thong so'tien do (nhan luc may moc)

Tién do phu thuoc ba loai thong so co ban d6 la cong nghe, khong gian va thoi gian. Thong s6 cong nghe bao gom s6 t6 doi (day chuyén) lam viec doc lap, khoi luong cong viec, thanh phan t6 doi (bien che), nang suat cua to doi. Thong so khong gian gom vi tri lam viec, tuyen cong tac va phan doan, dot thi cong. Thong so thoi gian gom thoi gian thi cong cong viec va thoi gian dua tung phan hay toan bo cong trinh vao hoat dong. Cac thong so gs uou tu op Aeyn ns ayo heuo heng Anb oyn nqu toa unb uat op u se lam cac thong so khac thay doi theo ya lam thay doi tien do thi cong. Viec chon cac thong so truoc tién phai phu hop v6i cong nghe thi cong sau d6 la hop ly ve mat to chuc. Tuy theo phuong phap to chuc nguoi ta chon cac thong so theo nhung nguyen tac rieng (se xem cu the sau trong cac truong hop riéng).

## 6. Xac dinh thoi gian thi cong

Thoi gian thi cong cong viec phu thuoc vao khoi luong, tuyen cong tac, muc do su dung tai nguyen va thoi han xay dung cong trinh. De day nhanh toc do xay dung, nang cao hieu qua co gioi hoa phai chu trong den che do lam viéc hai, ba ca, nhung cong viéc chinh dugc cd gioi hoa dong bo. Tuy nhien lam tang ca se lam tang phu phi nhu chieu sang, chi phi bao ho lam ca hai, ca ba, tang luc luong can bo ky thuat, quan ly. Nhung qua trinh thi cong thu cong chi ap dung lam tang ca khi khoi luong lon nhung tuyen cong tac hep khong trien khai them cong nhan duoc.

Khi dieu kien thi cong tuong doi chuan va on dinh, thoi gian thi cong cong viéc xac dinh theo bieu thuc (2.6), (2.7):

<!-- formula-not-decoded -->

CM, - so ca may de hoan thanh cong viec;

a - so ca lam viec trong ngay (che do lam viec);

Thoi gian thi cong dai nhat Tmax co duoc khi bo tri luc luong thi cong toi thieu voi nguon tai nguyen tuong ung ma cong viec khong bi dut doan. Vi vay, thoi gian dai nhat thi cong cong viéc khi chi bo tri mot may thi cong hay mot to san xuat co ban. O day ta hieu to san xuat co ban la don vi san

xuat toi uu voi so nguoi it nhat van dam bao nang suat chat luong tinh theo dau nguoi (to san xuat co ban thuong duoc ghi trong dinh muc ky thuat).

## 7. Lap tien do ban dau

Sau khi chon bien phap thi cong va xac dinh cac thong so to chuc, ta tien hanh lap tien do ban dau.

Lap tien do bao gom xac dinh phuong phap the hien tien do va thu tu cong

nghe hop ly trien khai cong viec.

Tien do c6 the the hien bang bieu do ngang, bieu do xien hay so d6 mang (SDM). Chon cach rao tuy thuoc vao quy mo, tinh phuc tap cua cong trinh. Bieu d6 ngang thuong bieu dien tien do cong trinh nho va cong nghe don gian. Bieu do xien dung the hien tien do thi cong doi h?i su chat che ve thoi gian va khong gian. Bieu do xien chi thich hop khi so luong cac cong viec it. SDM dung de the hien tien do thi cong nhung cong trinh lon va phuic tap.

Thu tu trien khai cong viéc luon gan lien voi thu tu thi cong. Ben canh chu y dén cong nghe, luon khai thac kha nang trien khai cong viec dong thoi song song de rut ngan thdi gian thi cong. Mat khac trien khai cong viéc can chu y den van de su dung tai nguyen va dam bao to doi chuyen mon hoat dong theo day chuyen.

Lap tien do ban dau co the tien hanh theo ba cach sau:

- a) Sap xep cong viec tu cong viec dau tién theo thu tu cong nghe den cong viéc ket thuc. Day la phuong phap kha ph6 bien, don gian song doi hoi dax des bn sonau supnu tenx ues wanyau quy o 1eyd del nau cong viec chu dao truoc dua vao d6 sap xep cac cong viec con lai theo cac m%i quan he ve t6 chuc. Tuy nhién, doi voi cong trinh lon lap theo cach nay rat kho di den ket qua mong muon.
- b) Sap xep cong viec theo cac thoi diem dinh. truoc di nguoc lai cac cong viéc phia truoc. Day la phuong phap lap theo muc dich dinh truoc, thudng nguoi ta lay thoi han hoan thanh cong trinh, thoi han ket thuc cac giai doan lam diem xuat phat tim nguoc lai nhung cong viec lien truoc. Cach nay chong dat muc tieu nhung mo hinh san xuat de so cung vi xac dinh cac thoi diem cong viec khong chinh xac lam cho cong viec bi bo cung.
3. ua deyd suonqd eI Aea 'op uan deI gp (as) ooy ueo yuny ou Suna ( dyd suonyd 'den onyd dpy auona sunqu sonp hanb 1e e dnia ua nay dau tién nguoi ta dung toan hoc (SDM) mo hinh hoa qua trinh xay dung cong trinh - nghia la dung so do mang de the hien nhung logic cong nghe va t6 chuc can thiet xay dung cong trinh, sau d6 dung cong cu toan (thuat toan SDM) de tinh toan cac thong so cua mo hinh san xuat

Thong thuong cong viec duoc the hien thoi diem bat dau (ky hieu S) va thoi diem ket thuc (ky hieu F). Nhung cong viéc co the kéo dai thoi han thi cong ngoai thdi gian da dinh ve tiep bang dudng dut net (- - - - -) vé phia sau (cong viec a) neu kéo dai thoi diem ket thuc, vé phia truoc (cong viec b) neu the hien kha nang bat dau som. Nhung cong viec co lien quan den nhau ve mat to chuc se noi voi nhau bang duong dut net (cong viec a. d, f). Nhung cong viec giu vai tro chu dao quyet dinh tien do thi cong (duong gang) dudc the hien bang duong dam net (a, d, f, e). Mot cong viec khi t6 chuc thi cong chia ra lam nhieu cong viec nh? duoc the hien bang mot duong c6 vach chia cat gua chung (cong viec C, C2, C3).

Tren duong the hien cong viec nguoi ta co the dua nhieu thong so khac nhau (nhu tai nguyen, nhan luc, vat lieu, may moc, tien...).

Bieu d6 tieu thu tai nguyen duoc ve duoi tien do. Thong thuong nguoi ta chi the hien cac tai nguyen quan trong nhu nhan luc, tien von, cac vat liéu dung nhieu (da, cat, gach, xi mang...) phai cung ung thuong xuyen. Muon ve duoc bieu do tai nguyen chinh xac nguoi ta phai the hien chung ngay tren tüng cong viec cua tien do. Sau khi ve xong tien do chi viéc cong tai nguyen theo chung loai tai cac thoi diem ta duoc bieu d6 (neu su dung chuong trinh co san "project" may se tu dong ve).

Nhd co bieu do tieu thu tai nguyen, ta co the lap bieu do cung ung hay lam cac bai toan toi uu tien do.


## 2.4. CAC NGUYEN TAC LAP TIEN dO

De tien do lap ra gan sat voi thuc te va yeu cau cua cong trinh, lam cho viéc xay dung cong trinh dat hieu qua kinh te ky thuat chung ta can dinh huong theo nhung nguyen tac co ban sau:


## 1. On dinh nhing cong viec chuan bi kip thoi de tien hanh thi cong xay dung chinh

Cong tac xay dung cong trinh tien hanh thuan lgi hay khong phu thuoc rat nhieu vao cong tac chuan bi. Thong thuong xay dung mot cong trinh nguoi ta chia ra lam hai phan: phan cong tac chuan bi va phan xay dung chinh. Phan chuan bi bao gom chuan bi mat bang va xay dung lan trai, duong sa tam thoi phuc vu viec thi cong, con phan xay dung chinh bao gom xay dung cong trinh va dua cong trinh vao hoat dong.

Nhung cong tac chuan bi mat bang bao gom: pha do di chuyen cac nha cua, cong trinh co trong pham vi cong truong; don dep, thoat nuoc, ton nen lam duong phuc vu thi cong. Tim nguon cung cap nang luong, nhien lieu, dién nuoc v.v..

![Page 32](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/ae5fc51b-b6f4-40b8-ba1e-3368a78d6aba.png)

---

s x o cac xuong san xuat, cac khu sinh hoat cua cong nhan tren cong truong lam viéc.

Kinh nghiem chi ra rang cong tac chuan bi tot se lam cong tac xay dung chinh se tien hanh nhanh va dat hiéu qua cao. Tuy nhien khong phai tat ca cac cong viec chuan bi trén tien do deu phai hoan thanh truoc khi kh?i cong xay dung. Khi lap tien do nguoi ta chi xac dinh dugc nhung viec phai lam truoc, nhung viéc phai lam cung voi cong viéc chinh. Viéc lua chon truoc sau dua tren nguyen tac la tao dieu kien tot nhat de lam cong tac thi cong chinh nhung phai ket hop su dung tai nguyen, nang luc san xuat mot cach hop ly, tiet kiém.

De giam phan xay dung tam khi lap tien do phai xac dinh duoc su ket hop s" dung cong trinh vinh cuu vao muc dich su dung tam, dac biet la phan xay dung duong xa, mang luoi ky thuat, nha cong ich.


## 2. Chon thut tu thi cong hop ly

Khi xay dung nhung cong trinh lon, phuc tap, co nhieu hang muc cong trinh lien quan voi nhau qua chuc nang, cong nghe, dieu kien thi cong nen d oex onp 1yd m nyn sunqu o '1 doy 1yd suoo yyn nn nyn uoyo 5a ngay khi thiet ke to chuc xay dung. Khi chon thu tu trien khai cong viec ta can chu y den nhung van de sau:

Nhung cong viec thi cong tuan tu phai theo cong nghe san xuat, no se dong quip taAnb ba guop teoy oga qur Suoo oq ueo Key ugud 3una enp on 1A thoi han thi cong. Neu thu tu khong bao dam se lam kéo dai thoi han thi cong hoac lam giam hieu qua kinh te cua du an.

Van de tap trung nhan luc, may moc vao tung viec trong diem cung vo cung quan trong. Nen tap trung luc luong vao dut diem nhung phan viéc auou uap eu suoni oni uen uqd uau guouy 'oep nyo yu go uos suox uo de lam kéo dai thoi han thi cong va von u dong lau.

Can quan tam den nhung hang muc cong trinh quyet dinh ket thuc thdi nhu cau, ham, nha cong nghiép.... rat kh6 khac phuc khi muon day nhanh tien do.

Nhung cum hang muc cong trinh trong mot day chuyen san xuat cung nen uu tién trien khai dong bo, de khi dua cong trinh vao hoat dong thi cac cong trinh phu trd cung san sang.

![Page 33](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/7400b4ec-d62b-471b-8eaa-8c9c126c6d54.png)

---

Trong tung cong trinh cac cong viec trien khai theo thu tu cong nghe hop ly nhat, co gang dam bao tinh lien tuc cho cac t6 thg chinh.

Thong thuong theo cong nghe nguoi ta trien khai cong viec nhu sau: Thi cong tu trong ra ngoai, phan ket cau thi cong duoi lén, tu he chinh sang he phu, ti he chiu luc sang he khong chiu luc, tü he on dinh sang he khong on dinh. phan hoan thien thi cong tu tren xuong, tu trong ra ngoai, tü dau nguon xuong cuoi nguon. Tuy nhien thu tu d6 khong phai luc nao cung the, no phai tuy theo cu the cua tung cong trinh va tinh chat cong nghe ta su dung.

Trien khai cong viec cung chu y den tinh hinh thoi tiet de loai tru gian doan do chung gay ra.

Viéc tap trung nhan luc phai chu y den nhung gioi han ve tai nguyén va dam bao nguyen tac dieu hoa trong t6 chuc.

Nhung cong viéc thi cong kh6 phai bo tri nhung bién phap h6 tro khi can thiet de giu duoc y d6 khi lap tien do.


## 3. Dam bao thoi han thi cong

Mot trong nhung muc dich quan trong cua thiet ke t6 chuc xay dung la bao dam thoi han thi cong phap lenh. Thoi han phap lénh do nghi dinh cua chinh phu ve quan ly xay dung quy dinh neu cong trinh do nha nuoc quan ly, neu cong trinh chu dau tu la doanh nghiép do doanh nghiép quy dinh. Du chu dau tu la thanh phan kinh te nao thdi han xay dung cong trinh chi goi la phap lenh khi duoc ghi trong hop dong giao thau. Thoi han hoan thanh dua cong trinh vao hoat dong vo cung quan trong. Doi khi no quyet dinh thang bai cua mot du an, vi vay'moi su thay d6i thoi han thi cong deu phai su theo cac dieu khoan ghi trong hop dong (thu?ng khi rut ngan, phat khi keo dai hoac dinh chi hop' dong).

Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS Thoi han xay dung duoc hieu la thoi gian thuc hien cong tac xay lap va dua De cong trinh hoan thanh dung thoi han phap lenh, tien do ban dau lap phai tuan theo dieu kien nay. Trong khi lap tien do, thdi han xay dung cong trinh phu thuoc vao thoi han thi cong cua tüng cong viec va su sap xep chung theo thoi gian. Vi vay nguoi lap tien do phai lam chu duoc cac cong viec va nam dugc quy trinh cong nghe thi cong toan cong trinh. Neu tién do lap theo cach a (xem muc 2.4) thdi han thi cong ban dau thuong sai léch voi thoi han phap lenh kha nhieu nen phai dieu chinh, lap theo cach b (xem muc 2.4) thoi han cua tien do ban dau gan v6i thoi han phap lénh hon.

Thuong tien do ban dau lap sau khi tinh toan se xay ra ba truong hop:

## 5. Dua tièn von vao cong trinh hgp ly

Von dau tu la luong tien bo vao cong trinh. Tien von la loai tai nguyen su dung mot lan, n6 chi sinh loi khi cong trinh hoat dong. Vi vay viec dua tien vao cong trinh la mot chi tieu quan trong cua mot tien do.

Khi thiet ke t6 chuc xay dung nguoi ta thuong xem xét bieu do cung cap suey ueau Kea Supnyi uoa Sunp Aex yonau 1oa loG 'ueq oo Sunp Kex uoA phai chiu mot lai suat. Nguoi xay dung chi tra duoc khi ben A tam ung hoac thanh toan hop dong. Truong hop ben A khong tam ung kip thoi thi ben chu thau phai chiu lai ngan hang. Vi vay tien dua vao cong trinh cang som thi cang de bi u dong gay nen thua thiet cho nguoi xay dung. Ngudi ta phai tim mot hinh thuc dua tien von vao cong trinh sao cho u dong thap nhat.

Hinh thuc dua tien von vao cong trinh co ba dang co ban duoc the hien trén hinh 2.5. Duong vi phan dua tien vao cong trinh the hien luong tien chi phi cho cac cong tac xay lap tai cac khoang thoi gian theo tien do (tuan, thang, quy, nam).

Hinh 2.5a - dua tien vao cong trinh deu dan, tü luc bat dau den luc ket thuc luong von dau tu trong khoang thoi gian nhu nhau bang nhau.

Hinh 2.5b - dua tien vao cong trinh tang dan, luc dau chi phi it sau tang dan len, luc ket thuc dua tien vao cong trinh cao nhat.

Hinh 2.5c - dua tien vao cong trinh giam dan, luc dau lon nhat sau giam dan, luc ket thuc luong tien dua vao it nhat.

De danh gia ba hinh thuc dau tu tren vao cong trinh nguoi ta ve bieu do tich phan von dau tu (duong tong chi). Tren hinh 2.6 ta c6 ba duong tich phan tuong ung voi ba cach dau tu ? tren.

Hinh 2.5. Hinh thuc dau tu vao cong trinh a) Dau tu deu; b) Dau tu tang dan; c) Dau tu giam dan.

![Page 37](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/75feb00b-a0ee-4cef-a61a-c4182efc82ae.png)

---

![Image](images/docling_9.jpg)

Hinh 2.6. Duong tich phan von dau tu vao cong trinh a) Dau tu deu ; b) Dau tu tang dan; c) Dau tu giam dan.

![Image](images/docling_10.jpg)

Duong tich phan the hien tong von dua vao cong trinh den cac thoi diem tren tien do. No the hien tong so tien dua vao thuc hien cong tac xay dung den thoi diem ta xet t. Hien nhien dang duong tich phan phu thuoc vao duong vi phan. Duong tich phan 2.6a la duong thang, duong 2.6b la duong parabol lom con 2.6c la duong parabol 16i. Vi ba hinh thuc dau tu cho cung mot cong trinh nen gia tri cuoi cung deu bang nhau (C). Dien tich phan gach giua duong tich phan va truc (T) the hien u dong tien von vao cong trinh. Muc dich cua nguoi lam kinh te la sao cho u dong von thap nhat- ro rang khi dudng tich phan co dien tich gach chéo be nhat.

Doi voi mot cong trinh du dau tu tien theo cach nao cung phai bao dam thoi han thi cong va gia thanh khong doi. Nghia la co chung (T) va (C). Vay voi phep tinh tich phan don gian ta tinh duoc:

<!-- formula-not-decoded -->

Nhu vay khi lap tien do thi cong cong trinh ta to chuc cac cong viec sao cho duong vi phan dau tu von dang tang dan de duong tich phan co dien tich nh? nhat.

Tuy nhién khi tien do lap theo duong tich phan c6 dien tich nh? nhat s o u e o o l6n se tang tru su nguy hiem khi tien do bi sai léch.

De chung minh cho khai niem tren ta xet hinh 2.7 the hien ke hoach xay dung mot cong trinh tien do lap theo so do mang. Hai duong cong Ca, C, la duong gioi han tich phan tren va duoi cua phuong thuc dau tu tien von vao cong trinh.

![Page 38](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/3ff0e591-b6a9-4ac1-93de-6eee8577f0a2.png)

Duong C, tuong ung truong hop tat ca cac cong viec bat dau va ket thuc voi thoi diem som. Duong C, tuong ung truong hop tat ca cac cong viec bat dau va ket thuc voi thdi diem muon nhat. ## 2.5. CAC PHUONG PHAP KIEm TRA VIEC THUC HIEN TIEN DO

De cong trinh xay dung theo dung ke hoach cac cong viec trong tien do phai the dan den nhung ket qua ngoai y muon. De danh gia kip thoi va co bien phap su ly dung nguoi ta phai tien hanh he thong kiem tra thuc hien tien do toan phan hay mot so cong viec. Co the kiem tra dinh ky hoac dot xuat.

Khi tien do thi cong the hien tren bieu d6 ngang, ta co the tien hanh kiém tra theo ba phuong phap:

- 1) Phuong phap duong tich phan:
- 2) Phuong phap duong phan tram:
- 3) Phuong phap bieu d6 nhat ky.

Khi tien do lap va the hien tren so d6 mang, phuong phap kiem tra se nghien cuu trong chuong 4.


## 1. Phuong phap duong tich phan dung de kiem tra ting cong viec (hinh 2.8)

![Page 39](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/8070611c-639e-4107-9e7f-bbb564b6060b.png)

---

Theo phuong phap nay truc tung the hien khoi luong cong viec, truc hoanh la thoi gian. Sau moi khoang thoi gian khoi luong cong viec da thuc hién den thoi diem d6 duoc dua lén truc toa do. Duong the hien cong viec thuc hien den cac thoi diem xét la duong tich phan. De so sanh voi tien do ta biet duoc tinh hinh thuc hien tien do.

Xét tai thoi diem (t) ta co duong (1) la duong ke hoach. Neu dudng thuc hien la duong (3) - thuc hien dung ke hoach, neu la (2) - hoan thanh som, neu la (4) - hoan thanh cham.

Neu muon biet toc do thuc hien cong viec ta dung lat cat (v) (song song voi truc thoi gian t), duong (2), cat truoc duong ke hoach (1) thuc hien nhanh (+△t), duong (4) cat sau thuc hien cham (-△t).

Phuong phap duong tich phan co uu diem cho ta biet tinh hinh thuc hien tién do hang ngay song co nhuoc diem la khoi luong cong viec phai thu thap thuong xuyen va moi loai cong viec phai ve mot duong tich phan. Vi vay no phu hop voi viéc theo doi thuong xuyen viéc thuc hien tién do.

Nguoi ta thuong ap dung cho nhung cong tac chu yeu, can theo doi chat che.

Hinh 2.8. Kiem tra tien do bang duong tich phan (1) - ke hoach; (2), (3), (4) - dudng thuc hien.

![Image](images/docling_12.jpg)


## 2. Phuong phap duong phan tram

Day la phuong phap ap dung kiem tra nhieu cong viec mot luc tren tien do thé hien bang so do ngang. Tren hinh 2.9 trinh bay cach kiem tra tien do bang duong phan tram.

![Page 40](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/6575375b-4ce4-424d-bad4-849d1c9fc4db.png)

---

Hinh 2.9. Kiem tra tien do bang duong phan tram (1) - Duong kiém tra; (2) -Duong phan tram (cong viec A va E khong xet)

![Image](images/docling_13.jpg)

Phuong phap thuc hien nhu sau: trén tien do biéu dien bang bieu d6 ngang. Moi cong viec duoc the hién bang mot duong thang co do dai 100% khoi luong cong viec. Tai thoi diem t bat ki can kiem tra nguoi ta ke mot duong thang dung, do la duong kiem tra. Tren tien do cac cong viec roi vao mot trong hai truong hop. Truong hop cac cong viec da ket thuc hoac chua bat dau khong cat duong kiem tra ta b? qua. Truong hop nhung cong viec dang thi cong - cat duong kiém tra - phai lay s6 liéu khoi luong da thuc hien tinh dén thoi diém d6. Theo phan tram toan bo khoi luong, s0 phan tram thuc hien duoc dua len bieu do, chung noi lai voi nhau tao thanh duong phan tram. D6 la duong thuc te thuc hien. Nhin duong phan tram ngudi ta biet duoc tinh hinh thuc hién tien do.

Neu duong phan tram ? ben phai lat cat - nhung viec d6 thuc hien vuot muc ke hoach; neu duong phan tram ? ben trai - cong viec thuc hien cham tre. Nhung diem ma duong phan tram trung voi lat cat - cong viec thuc hién dung ke hoach.

Day la phuong phap thuong ap dung trong kiem tra dot xuat. N6 giup cho ia s a h op


## 3. Phuong phap bieu d6 nhat ky (hinh 2.10)

Day la phuong phap kiem tra hang ngay cua tung cong viec. Theo ke hoach moi cong tac phai thuc hien mot khoi luong nhat dinh trong tung ngay lam viec (Phieu giao viec).

![Image](images/docling_14.jpg)

Chung duoc the hien bang mot duong ke hoach. Hang ngay sau khi lam viec khoi luong thuc hien cong tac dugc xac dinh va ve vao bieu do, ta dugc duong thuc hien. Qua bieu do ta biet duoc nang suat cua tung ngay vuot, dat, khong dat de dieu chinh cho cac ngay tiep theo. Phuong phap nay chinh xac kip thoi nhung ton thoi gian chi ap dung cho t6 doi chuyen mon, hoac nhung viéc doi hoi giam sat sit sao.

![Page 41](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/5b2a8c25-0820-4858-8a2c-5ccf9e848175.png)

---

Tom lai, cac phuong phap tren chi cung cap cho ta tinh hinh thuc hién tien unqd o uu ua o ou ss ns eno enb nu ea uuu uanu u o phap tren chua noi len duoc. De nam bat sau sac ta phai tien hanh phan fy nno uryau as uop p ea ea yuep deyd suonyd oo sunp de ea y cac phan tiep sau.

![Image](images/docling_15.jpg)


## TO CHUC XAY DUNG THEO PHUONG PHAP DAY CHUYEN


## 3.1. khAI NIEm ve cAc PhUonG PhAP tO Chuc SAn xuAT

Xay dung la mot nganh san xuat cong nghiep, vi qua san xuat xay dung nguoi ta da bien cac loai vat tu, vat lieu thanh san pham cho xa hoi la cac toa nha, cac cong trinh phuc vu quoc ke dan sinh.

De hoan thanh nhiem vu san xuat ngudi thuc hien cong viec xay lap phai tap hop du vat tu, may moc, thiet bi va dac biet con nguoi de tien hanh cong viec. Viéc tién hanh cong viéc san xuat co the thuc hién theo nhieu cach khac nhau. Ta goi la phuong phap t6 chuc san xuat.

Du phuong phap nao cung phai tuan theo cac nguyen tac co ban cua t6 chuc san xuat, dac biet la to chuc san xuat xay dung, d6 la:

- .Tuan thu cong nghe san xuat de bao dam chat luong cong trinh.
- ·Bao dam thoi han thi cong.
- .Ha gia thanh san pham.

Cho dén nay nguoi ta co the chia phuong phap t6 chuc san xuat xay dung thanh ba phuong phap chinh la: tuan tu, song song va phuong phap day chuyen. Moi phuong phap co nhung uu nhugc diem riéng, tuy theo cac dieu kien cu the cac phuong phap d6 duoc ap dung triet de hay ting phan hoac ket hop, deu voi mot muc dich la dua lai hieu qua san xuat cao nhat.

![Page 42](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/06765750-a3f0-48aa-98b7-57b81777bb6f.png)

## 3. Phuong phap to'chitc san xuat theo day chuyen

Hai phuong phap trén co uu va nhuoc diem trai nguoc nhau ve thoi gian thi cong va muc huy dong tai nguyen. Nhung deu c6 chung mot nhuoc diem la it quan tam den su lam viéc cua cac t6 thg ve phuong dien chuyen mon hoa va tinh lien tuc.

![Page 44](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/ab9634dc-f35a-4deb-9741-c4d41c406792.png)

---

De khac phuc nhung nhuoc diem va phat huy uu diem, cac nha to chuc san xuat dua ra phuong phap to chuc san xuat day chuyen.

Ban chat cua t6 chuc san xuat theo day chuyen duoc minh hoa tren hinh 3.3.

De thuc hien san xuat theo day chuyen nguoi ta chia cong trinh thanh nhung phan viéc co chuyen mon rieng biet. Moi phan viec (cong tac) rieng Aea nun uaiy ony aun guon uow uaknyo go (op) g hou onyo g oonp za1q cac t6 doi se thay nhau lan luot hoan thanh cong tac cua minh ti cong trinh (phan doan) nay sang cong trinh khac den het.

Trén hinh 3.3 vi du c6 bon cong trinh A, B, C, D c6 cung cong nghe xay lap. Ta phan cong nghé xay lap d6 thanh bon cong viéc c6 chuyén mon khac nhau. Bon cong tac nay dugc bon t6 (doi) 1, 2, 3, 4 thuc hien. Cac t6 tuan tu hoan thanh cong viéc cua minh theo cong nghe san xuat va di chuyen tu cong trinh A sang B, C va D.

Qua hinh 3.3 ta thay thoi gian T, hoan thanh xay dung ca bon cong trinh ngan hon t6 chuc tuan tu T, va dai hon t6 chuc san xuat song song T2:

## 2. Thong so' khong gian

![Page 47](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/0bb86e6b-a391-4b27-a35d-63f5c86549e8.png)

---

Khi lam viec moi t6 tho doi hoi co mot khong gian nhat dinh, n6 the hién qua cac thong so khong gian la vi tri cong tac, tuyen cong tac, phan doan va phan dot thi cong.

- ·Vi tri cong tac (f): la khoang khong gian nho nhat de nguoi cong nhan lam viec thoai mai. an toan co nang suat. Vi tri cong tac phu thuoc vao loai cong viec. dung cu thi cong va kha nang lan viec cua cong nhan. Khi to chuc san xuat ta chon vi tri cong tae vüa du la co hieu qua nhat. Chat qua giam nang suat de mat an toan; rong qua lam thoi gian thi cong bi kéo dai doi khi giam nang suat.
- Tuyen cong tac (F): la tap hop cac vi tri cong tac cua ting loai cong viéc. T'uyen cong tac c6 tuyen kin va tuyen mo. Tuyen m? la tuyen co the trien khai cong viec duoc ngay, con tuyen dong la tuyen chi trien khai cong viec khi cong viec di truoc da ket thuc. Khai niem ve tuyén dong, tuyen m? chi la tuong doi vi no khong nhung phu thuoc vao cong nghe san xuat ma con phy thuoc vao cach to chuc cua nguoi san xuat.
- Phan doan (j,m): la phan cong trinh ma mot to (doi) co the trien khai, hoan thanh cong viec tren do mot cach doc lap, phat huy het cong suat va khong can tr? cong viec cua nguoi khac. Trong to chuc san xuat day chuyen, viec chon phan doan la rat quan trong. no anh huong truc tiep den chat luong cung nhu toc do san xuat. Chon phan doan phai phu hop voi cong nghe, kien truc, ket cau va y do t6 chuc san xuat. Ve cong nghe phai bao dam cong viéc lam dat chat luong, khoi luong thi cong phai gon nhe de thuc hien, phu hop voi nang luc san xuat dinh trien khai. Ve kien truc phai dam bao duoc tham my, lien tuc cua duong net, ranh gioi ro rang. Ve ket cau phai dam bao nhung phan thi cong xong ket cau 6n dinh neu dung khong anh huong den kha nang chiu luc. Ve mat to chuc san xuat dam bao khoi luong cong viec vua voi nang luc san xuat va doan dugc goi la mot cong tac, no c6 the thi cong, tinh toan mot cach doc lap.

Phan dot thi cong: la khai niem khong gian theo chieu cao khi thi cong cong trinh co diem dung theo chieu cao. Khai niem dgt thi cong thuong ap dung khi lam nha nhieu tang hoac ket cau co chieu cao lon. Dot thi cong gan dong nhat voi khai niem ve tuyen cong tac kin, tuy nhien dieu chat che ma nguoi t6 chuc thi cong khong the b? qua, d6 la cac gian doan ky thuat ta can luu y.


## 3. Thong so'thoi gian

Day chuyen san xuat the hien mot qua trinh lam ra san pham, no luon luon chuyen dong. Viec chuyen dong do the hien qua cac thong so thoi gian: nhip, buoc, gian doan va thoi han cua day chuyen. Cac thong so thdi gian cho ta mo hinh hoa cac qua trinh san xuat va noi len tinh chat cua day chuyen san xuat do.

- ·Nhip cua day chuyen ty: la khoang thoi gian hoat dong cua day chuyén i tren phan doan j (hinh 3.4).

Hinh 3.4. Bieu do xien cua day chuyen ① ② ③ ④ - T6 doi san xuat.

![Image](images/docling_19.jpg)

Nhip cua day chuyen cung the hien chu ky hoat dong cua day chuyen trén moi phan doan (neu khoi luong cong viec tren cac phan doan deu nhau). Trong t6 chuc san xuat day chuyen viec chon nhip rat quan trong vi n6 anh huong den thoi han, nang suat cua day chuyen. Thong thuong nguoi ta chon nhip la so chan cua don vi thoi gian (ngay, tuan...) de khong lam lang phi thoi gian vao viec di chuyen t6 tho. Giua nhip va bien che cua t6 thg co moi quan hée mat thiet (xem chuong I1): xac dinh theo cong thuc (3.7).

## 3.3. CAC NGUYEN TAC CO BAN KHI TO CHUC SAN XUAT XAY DUNG THEO DAY CHUYEN

De phuong phap to chuc san xuat theo day chuyen co hieu qua nguoi ta dua ra mot so nguyen tac can tuan theo. Sau day la cac nguyen tac co ban.


## 1. On dinh chuyen mon hoa

Nghia la trong mot don vi san xuat (t6, doi) muc do chuyén mon hoa phai duoc on dinh. Moi t6 cong nhan trong qua trinh san xuat chi lam mot loai cong viec, khong co su thay d6i dung cu, phuong tien cung nhu vat lieu san xuat. Nguyen tac nay lam cho tay nghe cong nhan duoc nang cao lien tuc, chat luong cong viec duoc bao dam. Tuy theo don vi san xuat va tinh chat cua cong truong muc do chuyen mon hoa cua to doi co the khac nhau (chuyen sau, chuyen nghe, chuyen nhom) nhung phai 6n dinh khi day chuyen hoat dong.

![Page 51](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/23a87e64-a297-4b15-875b-d8f75c444f5f.png)

---

## 2. Day chuyen hoat dong lién tuc

Nghia la khi mot to hay doi cong nhan bat dau tham gia san xuat thi se hoat dong lién tuc tu phan doan nay sang phan doan khac den khi hoan tat loai cong viec chuyen mon no phu trach. Tren tien do tinh lien tuc cong viéc the hien bang mot duong lien tü dau den cuoi (hinh 3.8). Trong san xuat to thg khong c6 thoi gian nao phai nghi lam giua chüng. Khi day chuyen hoat dong lién tuc nhieu cong nhan cung nhu can bo chi dao khong hoan thanh khoi luong cong viec ma tien do da vach. San xuat lien tuc lam chq nguoi cong nhan phat huy tinh tu giac va tu chu hoan thanh cong viec hang ngay lam tang them tinh ky luat lao dong.


## 3. Day chuyen lam viec dieu hoa

Nghia la lam viéc voi nang suat on dinh, trong mot khoang thoi gian nhu nhau day chuyen tiéu thu mot khoi luong tai nguyen nhu nhau, san xuat ra so san pham bang nhau. Muon day chuyén co nang suat on dinh thi bién che cong nhan, trang bi may moc dung cu lao dong phai co dinh. Trén tien do tinh dieu hoa the hién bang bieu d6 tai nguyen bang phang (hinh 3.8) va nang suat I khong doi:

I = N.s = const ; do dinh muc s = const. Suy ra N = const (bién che co dinh).

![Image](images/docling_23.jpg)

![Image](images/docling_24.jpg)

Hinh 3.8. Day chuyen lien tuc va on dinh

![Page 52](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/5758b7c8-73e8-4bcb-8f9a-71269e013064.png)

Nghia la tren mat bang lam viec (phan doan) chi c6 mot t6 chuyen mon hoat dong. Nguyen tac nay bao dam cac to thg khong gay can tr? lan nhau lam anh huong den nang suat chat luong va an toan lao dong. Trong thuc te c6 mot hai t6 chuyen mon khac nhau cung lam viec tren mot phan doan ma khong lam anh hu?ng lan nhau, khi d6 ta khong quan niém d6 la cac o a y n a a day chuyen dien nuoc trong xay dung dan dung). Tren tien do nguyen tac khong chong chéo bieu hien neu ta dung mat cat doc ? bat cu thoi diem nao tren tien do, chi cat mot duong the hién cong viec trén mot phan doan (hinh 3.9). Neu tren phan doan nao trong mot thoi diem c6 hai duong cong viec thi tai phan doan d6 co su chong chéo ve t6 chuc- vi pham nguyen tac day chuyen.

## 3.4. ThE HIEn dAy ChUyEn TrEn SO dO

Trong chuong 2 chung ta da biet tien do co the the hien bang ba loai sd do: ngang, xien va mang. Khi t6 chuc san xuat theo day chuyen thi tien do cung c6 the trinh bay bang ba loai so d6 d6.

Khi the hien tien do co t6 chuc san xuat theo day chuyen ta phai lam noi bat moi quan he giua cac thong so khong gian, thoi gian cua cong nghe qua bon nguyen tac co ban cua no.


## 1. The' hien tren so do ngang

![Page 54](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/5f3d5e13-0216-43c1-9b91-c5a4770264b1.png)

---

Thong so cong nghe duoc the hien bang cac duong cong viec. Thong so khong gian c6 hai cach the hien: cach mot (hinh 3.1la) phan doan duoc the hien bang truc tung; cach hai phan doan duoc the hien bang nhung ghi chu ngay trén duong cong viéc (hinh 3.11b).

Tren hinh 3.1la ta thay cac day chuyen (cong viéc A, B, C, D) the hien bang cac duong giat bac. Moi bac la mot phan doan, do dai cac doan tren tüng bac la nhip cua day chuyen tren doan d6. Cong trinh c6 bao nhieu doan thi co bay nhiéu dong the hién cac bac. Tien do co bao nhieu cong viec thi co bay nhieu duong giat bac. Tien do the hien dai theo ca truc tung va truc hoanh. Tinh lien tuc cua cong viec duoc the hien bang cac duong noi dut net.

Hinh 3.11. Tien do day chuyen the hien bang bieu do ngang

![Image](images/docling_27.jpg)

Tren hinh 3.1lb truc tung khong the hien phan doan duoc ma the hién cong viéc, moi cong viec bieu dien bang mot duong thang, chieu dai cua moi duong the hién thoi han hoat dong cua t6 thg lam cong viéc d6. Tren tién do c6 bao nhieu loai cong viec c6 bay nhieu, duong the hien cong viéc (day chuyen thanh phan). Dé phan biet nhip cua day chuyen thanh phan nguoi ta ghi chu tren cac duong the hien. Vi du A, Az... A; la cong viéc A lam tréen cac phan doan 1,2,...j; do dai cua cac doan la nhip cua day chuyen tren cac doan d6.


## 2. The' hien bang so d6 xién (hinh 3.12)

Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS http://www.vietcons.org Tren so d6 thoi gian the hien bang truc hoanh con phan doan the hién ca hai huong khong gian va thoi gian tao thanh nhung duong xien. Cong viéc (day chuyen thanh phan) ky hiéu bang chi so i con phan doan la j. Hinh chieu cua cac day chuyen tren truc hoanh ung voi moi phan doan la nhip (t,) cua day chuyen tren phan doan d6. Trén bieu d6 ta thay buoc cua day. chuyen Ko; cung nhu buoc vao, buoc ra cua cac day chuyen tren tung phan doan. Do vay the hien day chuyen bang so do xién la ro rang va day du nhat. Tren so do ta thay su phat trien cua t6 thg va cac diem can chu y (bat dau, vao ra va ket thuc) khi dieu hanh san xuat. Chinh vi vay tien do san xuat theo day chuyen thuong the hien bang so do xién.

Hinh 3.12. Tien do day chuyén the hien bang bieu d6 xien Ko: - buoc cua day chuyen; Zli+1- gian doan giua cac day chuyen i va i + 1 &amp; phan doan j: Kivao, Kira - buoc vao, buoc ra cua day chuyen i&amp; phan doan j.

Khi the hien bang so d6 mang CPM (hinh 3.13) ta quy uoc moi cong viec la doan (vi du xay ? phan doan 1,2,3....) la cac cong viec doc lap nhung giua cac cong viec se quan he voi nhau ve cong nghe va t6 chuc. Mang se the hién cac moi quan he theo quy tac cua n6 (xem chuong 4). Sau khi dung ps aow nyu u qu quy u 1 hnx us yu snb boy qu o su d6 mang binh thuong. Khi the hien mot tien do t6 chuc san xuat theo day chuyen phan lon nguoi ta dung bien d6 xién. Vi tren d6 cac thong so ve khong gian, thoi gian va cong nghe the hien rat ro rang.
