## 3.5. PHAN LOAl DAY CHUYEN

Trong san xuat xay dung nguoi ta phan loai day chuyen can cu vao cac quan diem de ra.

Theo co cau va loai san pham day chuyen lam ra, ngudi ta co: day chuyen bj phan, day chuyen chuyen mon hoa, day chuyen cong trinh va day chuyen lien hop.

Theo dac tinh cua nhip day chuyen ta co: day chuyen nhip nhang, day chuyen nhip boi va day chuyen khong nhip nhang.

Theo thdi gian hoat dong ta co: day chuyen ngan han va day chuyen dai han.


## 1. Day chuyen bo phan

Day chuyen bo phan con co ten la day chuyen don hay day chuyen thanh Au uop uyd n o u ora weh auu ou anx ues uaano Aep 1i ud sang phan doan khac. San pham cua n6 la mot loai cong viéc hoan thanh nhu dao dat, xay, cot thep, d6 betong. Day chuyen don tuong ung voi mot t6 san xuat n6 bieu hién muc do chuyen mon h6a san xuat. Day chuyen don t6 chuc voi qua trinh san xuat don gian thi do chuyen mon cang cao. uoy Aey dea onyd zenx ues qu enb ioa onuo g uop uaanqo Aep 1ei oonaN hop thi muc do chuyen mon hoa cang giam nhung tinh da nang cua t6 thg cao hon. Viec chon muc do chuyén mon hoa cua day chuyen don phu thuoc vao quy mo cua cong trinh va to chuc cua don vi xay lap.

Trén bieu do day chuyen don the hien bang mot duong. Tu day chuyen don Kep el 1oa uoo op op upu dea onyd ugAnuo Kep naiuu onuo g e ionau chuyen thanh phan hay bo phan.

Kex daiyau yx nou Key pua top ioa gun suona eoy uou uaknqo uaanyo Aea dung nh? nhu mong, than, hoan thién...

Day chuyen chuyen mon hoa c6 the hoat dong doc lap ve mat quan ly san xuat khi don vi xay lap co muc do t6 chuc chuyén mon hoa cao.

## 8. Day chuyen ngan han

Day chuyen ngan han la day chuyen to chuc de xay dung nhung cong trinh day chuyen loai nay thap.


## 9. Day chuyen dai han

![Page 61](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/bf18715d-327e-45e8-baf8-5e71da9aba15.png)

Day chuyen dai han t6 chuc hoat dong trong nhieu nam thuong bao gom mot phan hay ca chuong trinh san xuat cua don vi xay lap. Khi t6 chuc san xuat dai han nhung uu diem cua phuong phap day chuyen the hien ro nét. N6 duoc ap dung ? don vi san xuat lon co ke hoach dai han.


## 3.6. SU KHAC NHaU GIUa DAY CHUyEn XAY DUNG vA DAY CHUYEn CONG NGHIEP, uu NHUoC dIEm CUa PHUONG PHAP TO CHUC XAY DUNG THEO DAY CHUYEN

Phuong phap to chuc san xuat day chuyen ra doi trong san xuat cong nghiep. Ngay tu khi ra doi n6 da mang lai hieu qua kinh te rat lon: lam cho chat luong tang len do chuyen mon hoa cao do va nang suat tang do t6 chuc chat che.

Khi dugc van dung vao t6 chuc san xuat xay dung nhung nguyen tac co ban van duoc giu nguyen. Song do san xuat xay dung co nhung dac thu rieng lam cho day chuyen trong xay dung co nhieu diem khac biet. Ngudi can bo chi dao san xuat xay dung phai nam duoc thi moi phat huy duoc hieu qua. Cu the nhu sau:

Trong day chuyen cong nghiép nguoi va dung cu may moc dung yén, san pham di chuyen theo day chuyen ti vi tri nay sang vi tri khac, con trong xay dung nguoc lai, san pham dung yen con nguoi va may moc di chuyen theo mat bang san xuat. Nhu vay day chuyen xay dung la day chuyen doc con day chuyen cong nghiep la day chuyen ngang. Su khac biet nay dan den mot loat van de khac biet tiep theo la:.

Trong xay dung khong co su un tac cong viéc ma chi co hien tuong chong chéo to chuc (neu khong bao dam nguyen tac thu 3) va hay gap hien tuong b? trong mat bang (gian doan to chuc). Dieu chinh su suon se trong xay dung thuong dieu chinh khoang cach thoi gian giua cac day chuyen.

## 3.7. CAC BUOC LAP DAY CHUYEN XAY DUNG

Lap mot day chuyen san xuat chinh la lap ke hoach san xuat the hien qua tien do trong d6 nguoi ta chu trong ap dung cac nguyen tac cua day chuyen. Thong thuong, khi lap mot day chuyen xay dung ta tien hanh thuc hién cac buoc nhu sau: (the hien qua so d6 khoi trinh bay tren hinh 3.18).

Buoc I: Phan tich cong nghe xay dung dua tren nhitng quyet dinh cia thiet ke thi cong

Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS Suoo sunp Kex gp sunp de gu4 9o 4enyn fy yur Anb Sunqu yon ueyd trinh. Tim nhung moi lien he ve cong nghe bat buoc nguoi t6 chuc san xuat phai tuan theo trong d6 chu y den nhung lien hé, gian doan ky thuat. Nhung may moc thiet bi se su dung khi thi cong.

## Buoc 2: Xac dinh mic do chuyen mon hoa de' lap danh muc cong viec

Tren co s? phan tich dac diem cong nghe ta chia qua trinh xay dung nha (cong trinh) thanh cac qua trinh thanh phan. Cac qua trinh thanh phan phai thi cong doc lap duoc ? cac vi tri khac nhau trén cong trinh. Chung dugc tien hanh theo mot trinh tu cong nghe nhat dinh. Tuy theo quy mo cua cong trinh ma cac qua trinh thanh phan c6 muc do phan nh? khac nhau. Tu phan nh? tüng phan den phan nh? toan phan tuong ung voi cac qua trinh phuc tap hay don gian, n6 quyet dinh muc do chuyen mon hoa cua to tho. Ket qua phan chia

cong viec ta lap duoc danh muc cong viec thi cong.


## Buoc 3: Xac dinh cac thong so' cong nghe

Thanh lap cac to san xuat co chuyen mon tuong ung voi cac qua trinh san xuat tao thanh nhung day chuyen thanh phan. Cac day chuyen thanh phan nay se thuc hien phan viec cua minh tuan tu lien tuc tü vi tri nay sang vi tri tiep theo. Tuy theo muc do phan chia ra cac qua trinh san xuat ma co muc do chuyen mon hoa cua cac to doi tuong ung: t6 doi chuyen mon, hon hop hay da nang. Tinh chuyen mon va tinh da nang cua to thg la hai mat cua van de to chuc san xuat. Phat huy mat nao de dat dugc hieu qua san xuat la su tai tinh cua nguoi can bo t6 chuc san xuat. Sau d6 xac dinh bien che va nang xuat cua to doi theo cach da biet.


## Buoc 4: Xac dinh thong so khong gian

Trén cd s? phan tich dac diem rieng cua cong trinh (kien truc, ket cau, cong nang va cong nghe thi cong) ta phan chia mat bang san xuat ra cac phan khu. Su phan khu phai phu hop ve ky thuat va to chuc thi cong. Ve ky thuat phai bao dam tinh kha thi, bao dam chat luong. Ve t6 chuc phai dam bao khoi luong de viec thuc hien thuan loi va co nang suat.

Tren co so phan khu hop ly ta xac dinh duoc khoi luong cong viéc trén moi phan doan, neu khoi luong dong deu ta se c6 day chuyen nhip nhang va nguoc lai. Do do, viec van dung cac yeu to ky thuat de viec phan doan cang dong deu cang giam nhe cho viec to chuc thuc hién cua day chuyen.

## Buoc 6: Dung day chuyen tutc lien ket cac day chuyen theo thoi gian va khong gian

Sau khi da xac dinh duoc cac thong so cua day chuyen viec dung day

chuyen la su lien ket cac day chuyen don lai voi nhau tao thanh day chuyen san xuat. Viec lien ket cac day chuyen don bieu hien qua cac thong so khong gian va thoi gian dua trén hai nguyen tac: cong nghe va to chuc day chuyen.

Nguyen tac cong nghe la cac day chuyen ghep sat voi nhau theo thu tu cong nghe, bao dam cac quy trinh ky thuat de phuong an co tinh

kha thi dat chat luong.

Ve nguyen tac t6 chuc: tuan thu theo nguyen tac co ban thu ba va thu tu cua to chuc day chuyen trong xay dung la ghep sat toi han va khong chong chéo. Dam bao hai nguyen tac nay lam cho day chuyén

hoat dong troi chay va rut ngan thoi han thi cong.

Dieu nay thuc te thuong gap. neu khong giai quyet mau thuan nay day chuyen tr? nen cung nhac mat y nghia thuc te, dieu ma nhung nha to chuc day chuyen thuan tuy ly thuyet thuong hay mac phai.


## Buoc 7: Tinh cac chi so (tieu chi) cua day chuyen

Tai buoc 6 ta da co mot day chuyen san xuat (xay dung cong trinh) mot cach so bo theo cong nghe va t6 chuc dat ra. Thuong day chuyen so bo lap duoc chua dap ung ngay nhung tieu chi cua nha xay dung. De danh gia no ta tien hanh tinh cac chi tieu dat ra. Cac chi tieu can tinh la thoi han hoat dong cua day chuyen, tinh on dinh, tinh diéu hoa, nang suat, nhu cau ve tai nguyen trung binh va thoi diem cao nhat...


## Buoc 8: Danh gia cac chi tieu cua day chuyen da lap

Cac chi so cua day chuyén da lap can duoc danh gia bang cach so sanh voi cac tieu chi dat ra (cua chu dau tu, nha xay dung). Neu cac tieu chi co ban dat thi day chuyen lap duoc co the tr? thanh tien do phap lenh (Khi duoc hai ben A va B phe duyét).

Neu cac chi so khong thoa man cac tiéu chi de ra thi day chuyen lap duoc phai dieu chinh cho phu hop theo cac quy trinh sau:

- Vong 1: Dung lai day chuyen bang cach thay doi su lien ket ve khong gian va thdi gian khi deu kien cho phép. Qua d6 cac chi so cua day chuyen san xuat thay doi do vi tri cua cac day chuyen thanh phan thay doi nhung so luong va chat luong cua no van giu nguyén. Do d6 vong nay ta goi la vong dieu chinh t6 chuc neu bang vong 1 van khong dat, ta phai tien hanh dieu chinh theo vong 2.
- Vong 2: Dieu chinh nang suat cua cac day chuyen bang cach tang giam bien che ngudi, may m6c cua t6 san xuat. Neu bang vong hai van chua dap ung tieu chi dat ra ta phai tien hanh dieu chinh bang vong 3.
- Vong 3: Dieu chinh phuong an t6 chuc ky thuat bang cach thay doi muc d6 chuyén mon h6a t6 doi san xuat dan den thay d6i so day chuyén thanh phan hoac thay d6i cach thuc phan chia khong gian (doan, dot) lam cho day chuyen san xuat thay d6i l6n vi cac thong so co ban thay doi. Vong nay co the thay d6i phan nao hinh thuc va ban chat cua day chuyen do vay ta cung c6 the noi day la vong thiet ke lai day chuyen san xuat.


## Buoc 9: Chuyen sang tien do phap lenh

Khi cac tiéu chi dat ra duoc day chuyén da lap dap ung thi day chuyen san xuat d6 co the chuyen sang tien do phap lenh (khi duoc hai ben nhat tri dua vao noi dung hop dong kinh te - ky thuat).

![Page 68](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/77536f76-fdc2-4c95-b97f-c97c72fd5e41.png)

---

Day chuyen duoc chuyen sang tien do bang cach gan no voi nien lich nhat dinh. Cac thoi diem cua cong viec (kh?i, ket) thdi gian thi cong duoc the hien tren truc thoi gian (lich) d6. Cac day chuyen thanh phan duoc the hien bang cac duong bat dau tu kh?i diem lam viéc

lien tuc 6n dinh den khi xong phan viec cua minh. Do d6 viec giao ke hoach cho cac to san xuat rat don gian va thuan tien.

Khi day chuyen san xuat da thanh tien do, moi viec thay d6i ve dieu hanh cong viec mot cach tuy tién la khong duoc phép. Chi nhung ngudi nam vung day chuyen do moi c6 the dieu chinh cuc bo bang cach tan dung nhung gian doan co trong day chuyen.


## Buoc 1o: Lap cac bieu do cung itng tai nguyen

Nhu moi ke hoach san xuat, viec cung ung tot la dieu can thiet dé tién do hoat dong dung ke hoach, nhat la khi t6 chuc san xuat theo day chuyen. Vi t6 chuc day chuyen rat chat che nen dieu kien cho n6 vat tu, nhan luc, may moc, thiet bi, nang luong va tien von v.v... Nhung bieu d6 cung ung tai nguyen cung la mot tieu chi danh gia su hop ly cua day chuyen.

## 3.13. DAY CHUYEN CONG TRiNH dON V!

Nguoi ta to chuc day chuyen de xay dung mot cong trinh khi khoi luong cong viec (cong trinh) tuong doi lon du dam bao day chuyen phat trien va 6n dinh Buo aunqu gunp Aex 1y dea auony Keu doy guona 'uee yu aou Suo1 trinh cong nghiep co mat bang lon hay nha nhiéu tang mat bang rong. N6i chung day chuyen cong trinh don vi la day chuyen ngan han.

Sunp ugA 9o aunp Kex onuo g4 gu4 oo yyo e 1eI oonau doy suonn Sunqu SuouL nhung uu diem cua day chuyen. D6 la cach t6 chuc mot s6 t6 chuyen mon h6a, hay van dung day chuyen cuc bo tung thoi diem (vi khoi luong, mat bang khong du lon de day chuyen hoat dong theo day du y nghia cua no). Nhung truong hop nay rat hay gap khi t6 chuc xay dung nhung nha nhieu tang khong lon.

De xay dung nhung ngoi nha don lé theo phuong phap day chuyen nguoi ta to chuc cac day chuyen chuyen mon hoa gom mot so day chuyen thanh phan. Moi day chuyen thanh phan tuong ung voi mot t6 san xuat theo muc do chuyen mon nhat dinh. Cac day chuyen chuyén mon hoa lién ket voi nhau theo cong nghe va to chuc sao cho cac cong viec la lién tuc. Tap hop cac day chuyen chuyén mon gan bo huu co voi nhau tao thanh day chuyen cong trinh, san pham cua no la mot ngoi nha hoan chinh.

Khi xay dung nhung ngoi nha nhieu tang day chuyen cong trinh gom bon day chuyen chuyen mon hoa chinh tuong ung voi bon giai doan cong nghe xay dung. Day chuyen I la phan ngam, day chuyen II la phan ket cau than nha, day chuyen IHl - cong tac hoan thien, day chuyen IV - cong tac mai. Neu la nha cong nghiep ta co the them day chuyen V . lap dat he thong thiet bi, ky thuat.

Day chuyen chuyen mon trong day chuyen cong trinh xay dung nha cong nghiep mot tang gom:

- I - Day chuyen phan ngam.
- HI - Day chuyen phan than (ket cau chiu luc va bao che)
- IHI - Cong tac hoan thien.
- IV . Lap dat thiet bi cong nghe.

Viec phan chia cong trinh ra cac phan doan mot mat phu thuoc vao giai phap mat bang, ket cau cong trinh, mot mat phu thuoc vao cong nghe va muc dich, t6 chuc thi cong.

Trong xay dung nha cong nghiep mot tang phan doan thuong lay mot vai o o g (hinh 3.39)

![Page 110](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/3d68f338-4e7b-44b1-97f3-0c8e3d1dd5fb.png)

Trong xay dung nha nhieu tang phan doan thuong chon don nguyen tren moi tang nhung cung co the lay hai don nguyen tren cung mot tang hoac hai tang u 1 o o os uoo (otg qu) unu up ho uo phan doan cua to thg la de dang.

Cach chia phan doan co the thong nhat cho tat ca cac day chuyen chuyén mon nhung cung c6 the khac khi so phan doan cua cac day chuyen chuyen mon khac nhau. S6 thu tu cua phan doan cung co the thong nhat nhung cung co the khac nhau.

Khi chia phan doan co gang sao cho khoi luong cong viéc tren cac phan doan xap xi bang nhau (sai s0 duoi 20%) de c6 the t6 chuc day chuyen nhip nhang.

- a) Phan doan ddn nguyen trong mot tang;
- b) Phan doan hai tang trong mot ddn nguyen;
- c) Phan doan hai don nguyen trong mot tang.

Su phat trien cua cac day chuyen chuyen mon hoa, su lien ket giua chung ve thoi gian va vi tri phu thuoc vao hoat dong cua cac day chuyén. No phy thuoc vao su phan chia phan doan va so do di chuyen cua cac doi tho.

Huong phat trien cua cac day chuyen chuyen mon hoa co the ngang hoac dung (hinh 3.41).

Khi xay nha cong nghiep mot tang huong phat trien cua cac day chuyen la ngang. Cac day chuyen phan ngam (l) va mai (IV) co the phat trien theo chiéu ngang nha va doc nha, day chuyen phan than (Hl) thuong la doc nha.

Trong nha nhieu tang day chuyen chuyén mon phan than dai bo phan thuc hién theo sd d6 ngang duoi léen, rat it trudng hop dung so d6 dung duoi len di ngang.

Day chuyen hoan thien (IIl) huong phat trien rat khac nhau. Khi s6 tang nhieu de giam thdi gian gian doan do t6 chuc giua day chuyen phan than va than (ngang duoi lén). Trong cac truong hop con lai cho day chuyen hoan thien phat trien theo huong dung tren xuong de duoc mai bao ve hoac dung du6i len co su bao ve cua hai tang san.

chuyen chuyen mon lai voi nhau theo thoi gian va khong gian.

## 3.16. DIEU KIEN AP DUNG PHUONG PHAP TO CHUC SAN XUAT DAY CHUYEN TrONG XAY DUNG

T6 chuc san xuat theo day chuyen la phuong phap tien tien. Ap dung day chuyen trong xay dung mang lai hieu qua rat to lon. Thuc tien ? cac nuoc cong nghiep phat trien da chung minh dieu d6. Xong de phuong phap t6 chuc day chuyen co hieu qua thi san xuat phai dap ung nhung dieu kien sau:

- .Viec to chuc san xuat phai ro rang, chinh xac theo cac yeu cau cua nguyen tac co ban to chuc day chuyen.
- Dam bao cung ung day du so luong va chuyen mon cac t6 doi cong nhan,
- may moc thiet bi, phuong tien van tai.
- Cung ung vat tu, ky thuat theo dung bieu do yeu cau.
- C6 ke hoach dai han bao dam khoi luong cong viec de day chuyen hoat dong lau se mang lai hieu qua cao. Trong truong hop khong du khoi luong (cong trinh don lé) viec ap dung day chuyen chi co tinh tan dung
- trong tung giai doan tung phan viec thi hieu qua rat it.
- Viec thong nhat hoa, cac giai phap ket cau, mat bang, nghia la dien hinh hoa cac thiet ke tao dieu kien cho viec t6 chuc day chuyen nhieu
- cong trinh cung loai hoat dong dai han.
- Cong nghiép hoa san xuat xay dung tao dieu kien cho to chuc day
- chuyen nhip nhang.
- Xay dung theo phuong phap day chuyen doi hoi phai kiem tra chi dao hang ngay. Phai co bo may dieu do de bao dam su ket hop nhip nhang
- an khop giüa cac don vi cung tham gia day chuyen san xuat.
- Cac don vi, ca nhan tham gia trong day chuyen phai duoc giai thich can ke cong viéc, khoi luong, thoi gian phai hoan thanh theo dung ke hoach de ra va y nghia cua viec tuan thu nghiem ngat ky luat san xuat.
- Viéc tra luong cho cong nhan nen ap dung co che khoan san pham theo don vi khoi luong la phan doan cong tac de khuyén khich viéc hoan thanh dung nhip dieu san xuat.

Thuc hien day du nhung dieu kien tren dam bao cho san xuat dat hien qua

cao nhat.

Tuy nhien trong thuc te san xuat. Khong phai luc nao cung hoi du cac dieu kién cua day chuyen de ra. Trong nhung truong hop do nguoi lap ke hoach cung nhu dieu hanh san xuat phai nang dong sang tao de co the ap dung dudc phudng phap t6 chuc tien tien nay, neu khong duoc toan bo co the áp dung tung phan (tung loai cong viec) hay tung giai doan thi hieu qua van dat dudc muc do nhat dinh.

## 4.1. KHAI NEM

Thong thuong trong xay dung nguoi ta su dung phuong phap cua Gant (so d6 ngang) de lap, biéu dien, dieu hanh va quan ly tien do thi cong. Ngoai viéc don gian tién loi, sd d6 ngang co nhung nhuoc diem sau:

![Page 126](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/30978f63-6535-4c82-8bbe-8d124e93ba99.png)

---

- Khong the hién ro va chat che moi quan hé ve cong nghe va t6 chuc giua cac cong viec
- thanh dung thoi gian (phap lénh) cua tien d?
- Khong cho phép bao quat duoc qua trinh thi cong nhung cong trinh phuc tap
- Dé b? sot cong viec khi quy mo cong trinh lon
- Kh6 du doan duoc su anh huong cua tien do thuc hien tung cong viec den tien do chung.
- nu oonp ou oe on g op u nau uo tn u uo nhan va giai phap khac phuc.

De khac phuc duoc cac nhuoc diem tren, khi xay dung cac cong trinh phuc tap, quy mo lon, nguoi ta dung sd d6 mang (SDM) de lap tien do thi cong.

So d6 mang la ten chung cua nhieu phuong phap nhu CPM, MPM, PERT. So d6 mang ra doi vao nhung nam cuoi cua the ky 19, dugc hoan thien va ap dung ? My vao nam 1958. Co s? toan hoc cua so d6 mang la ly thuyet d6 thi.


## So do mang c6 nhung uu diem sau:

- . Cho thay moi quan he chat che ve cong nghe, t6 chuc giua cac cong viéc.
- Chi ra duoc nhung cong viec quan trong, quyet dinh den thoi han hoan thanh cong trinh (cac cong viéc nay nhu sau nay goi la cong viéc "gang"). Do d6 nguoi quan ly biet tap trung chi dao co trong diem.
- .Loai tru duoc nhung khuyet diem cua so do ngang.


## PHUONG PHAP LAP VA DIEU HANH TIEN DO THI CONG THEO SO DO MANG

- Giam thoi gian tinh toan do su dung duoc may tinh dien tu vao lap, tinh, quan ly va dieu hanh tien do.

op ua fi uenb ea yuey nap 'op uan dei uon as aunp ns g ony eno trong thi cong cho thay hieu qua ro rét. Vi du:

O Lien Xo truoc day. do ap dung so do mang trong lap, dieu khien, quan ly tien do thi cong theo SDM khi xay dung nha may nghien than Stalingrat, da rut ngan thoi gian thi cong 12 thang, tiet kiem 2,4 trieu rup. Khi xay dung u p s s o osn n ae cong 25%.

![Page 127](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/c4c8601f-1fc2-4ab0-b0ca-c1f1346f15fb.png)

---

O nuoc ta, mac du viec su dung SDM con cham tre, song chung ta da thay ro duoc hieu qua cua viéc su dung SDM trong lap tien do va quan ly dieu hanh tien do trong xay dung cung nhu trong lap ke hoach cua cac nghanh khac.


## 4.2. DAI CUONG VE SO DO MANG CPM

Gia su lap tien do thi cong lap ghép khung nha bang ket cau thep, mong betong cot thép duc tai cho thi cong theo phuong phap thu cong. Khung thép dugc lap dung tai cong truong bang can truc, cau kien dugc van chuyen den mat bang truoc khi lap ghep. De don gian, chung ta gop lai thanh nam cong viec: thi cong mong, van chuyen can truc den cong truong, lap dung can truc, van chuyen ket cau khung den cong truong, lap ket cau khung. Gia su thoi gian thuc hien tuong ung voi cac cong viec tren la: 5; 1; 1;4; 8.

Neu lap tien d6 thi cong theo so d6 ngang, ta c6 tien do nhu bang 4.1.

## 4.3. CAC BO PHAN CUA SDM


## 1. Cong viec

Cong viec la mot hay tap hop qua trinh lao dong can phai chi phi thoi gian va tai nguyen thi cong.

Tren SDM, cong viec dudc bieu dien bang mui ten lien nét khong co ty lé lién quan den thoi gian thuc hien cong viec. Phia tren mui ten ghi ten cong viéc, phia duoi mui ten ghi thoi gian thuc hien cong viéc.

![Page 129](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/2cc5d4a1-e627-4411-846e-ec1b055b94c1.png)

---

Vi du: cong viec lap can truc ? hinh 4.2.

Hinh 4.2. Sd d6 mang (SDM) cua tien do thi cong

![Image](images/docling_68.jpg)


## 2. Su cho dgi

Su cho doi la mot qua trinh thi cong can chi phi thoi gian ma khong can chi phi tai nguyén thi cong.

Vi du: cong viéc cha be tong dat cuong do (de thao van khuon)

Thuc chat cong viec cho doi la cac gian doan ky thuat. Tren SDM, su cho doi duoc bieu dien bang mui ten lien net voi cac chi dan vé thdi han va ten cua su cho doi.


## 3. Su phu thuoc (cong viec ao)

viéc. N6 noi lén rang su kh?i cong cua cong tac nay phu thuoc vao su ket thuc cua cong tac kia.

![Page 130](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/ba13965a-f23d-4ccb-a749-a0d81b600051.png)

---

Vi du: tren hinh 4.1- cong tac lap khung chi duoc bat dau khi cac cong viec lam mong, van chuyen ket cau khung, lap can truc deu hoan thanh. Cong tac ao co thoi gian va chi phi tai nguyen bang khong.


## 4. Su kien

La moc danh dau su bat dau hay su ket thuc cua mot hoac vai cong viec (de cong viec tiep theo co the bat dau duoc)

Trong SDM, su kien duoc biéu dien bang vong tron co danh so thu tu.

Su kién luon c6 hai y nghia: doi voi cong viec lien truoc thi n6 la moc danh dau su ket thuc cong viec. Doi voi cong viec lien sau thi no la moc danh dau su bat dau cong viec (lien sau) d6.

Su kien. khong co cong viec lien truoc la su kien bat dau (kh?i cong). Su kién khong co cong viéc lién sau goi la su kien ket thuc SDM (su kien hoan thanh sau cung).


## 5. Duong trong SDM

Duong trong SDM la mot chuoi lien tuc cac cong viec tren SDM theo huong xac dinh (chieu mui ten). Chieu dai cua duong bang tong thoi gian thuc hien cua cac cong viec d6.

s o o o p o i i r s o t dau (khoi cong) den su kien i. Mot su kién co the co mot hay nhiéu duong lién truoc. Vi du tren hinh 4.3, su kién 1 co mot duong lien truoc 0-1-3-4-5.


## Duong lien sau su kien

Duong lien sau su kien i la mot day lien tuc cac cong viec di tu su kien i den su kien hoan thanh (ket thuc) SDM . Mot su kien co the co mot hay nhieu duong lien sau. Vi du trén hinh 4.3, su kien 5 co mot duong lien sau duong 5-6. Su kien 2 c6 3 duong lien sau la 2-4-6, 2-5-6, 2-4-5-6. Duong noi hai su kien i-r la tap hop lien tuc cac cong viec theo huong xac dinh tü su kien i den su kien r.

## 2. Cac buoc lap tien do theo phuong phap SDM (hinh 4.8)

Buoc 1: Phan tich cong nghe thi cong xay dung cua cong trinh.

- Buoc 2: Phan chia lap bien danh muc cong viec.
- Buoc 3: dua trén ket qua phan tich cac buoc 1, 2, 3, ta xac dinh cac moi quan he bat buoc giua cac cong viec. Quan he chu yeu la ket thuc cong viec truoc - bat dau cong viec sau (F - S). N6 duoc chia lam hai loai: quan he cong nghe va quan he to chuc. Quan he cong nghe dua theo quy trinh thi cong cong viec. Quan he t6 chuc ta chi dua vao nhung quan hé mang tinh to chuc khong the khong dua vao. Vi thieu se lam cho phuong an thi cong thay doi (vi du viec su dung chung mot can truc hay may bom be tong cho hai cong viec a va b la bat buoc thi dieu kién phai dua vao la b bat dau sau khi a ket thuc mac du giua a va b khong co moi lien he cong nghe. Trai lai nhung y d6 to chuc khong c6 ly do bat buoc ta khong dua vao de SDM se linh hoat hdn, mo hinh san xuat se mem hon, no sé tao dieu kien cho nguoi dieu hanh nang dong trong san xuat.
- Buoc 4: Xac dinh khoi luong cong viec theo danh muc da lap.
- Buoc 5: lap so d6 mang ban dau: can cu moi quan hé thiet lap 3 buoc 4, van dung cac nguyen tac ve SDM, ta ve SDM ban dau. Yeu cau cua so do mang ban dau la the hien het cac cong viec voi day du cac moi quan he bat buoc.

![Image](images/docling_74.jpg)

- Buoc 6: so chinh SDM: thuong SDM ban dau vüa lap chua co hinh dang don gian, ro rang. De co SDM hop ly ta tien hanh don gian hoa SDM ban dau. Truoc tien loai tru nhung su kien, nhung moi lien he thua bang cach nhap nhieu su kien giong nhau lam mot, loai b? nhung moi lien he (thuong la cong viec ao) trung lap. Tiep sau la di chuyen nhung su kien co the de giam su cat nhau giua cac cong doi de nhin, khoang cach giua cac su kien vua phai de ghi cac so liéu can thiet phu hop cho tinh toan (xem vi du hinh 4.9).
- Buoc 7: xac dinh cac thong so cua SDM. Day la buoc quan trong (co the thuc hien bang may tinh) xac dinh cac thong so can thiet theo cac quy tac se trinh bay ? cac muc sau.
- g g oo a o o s os dich). Thong thuong nguoi ta quan tam dau tien la do dai duong gang, sau d6 la cac chi so ve tieu thu tai nguyen, tuy theo muc dich cua ting cong trinh. Neu dat tiéu chi ta chuyen sang buoc 9 neu khong dat ta phai quay lai theo vong 1, 2, 3, 4.
- Vong 1: quay lai buoc 4 de dieu chinh cac chi so t6 chuc, tang giam nhan luc, may moc, t6 chuc lai cac t6 de thay d6i thoi gian thi cong. Cac buoc 5, 6, 7, 8 lap lai. Néu vong 1 khong dat chi tieu ta chuyen sang vong 2.
- Vong 2 : quay lai buoc 3 kiem tra lai cac moi quan he da dua vao, tim kiem nhung moi quan hé khong gay anh huong lon den cong nghe thi cong (khong bat buoc) hoac co the thay d6i duoc de giai phong SDM kh?i nhung rang buoc d6. Két qua ta duoc mot SDM moi cac buoc tiep theo duoc lap lai de tinh toan thong so moi.

Nhu vay vong 1 va 2 chi thay d6i trén SDM, s0 cong viéc khong co gi thay d6i so voi ban dau. Hai vong nay la thay doi cach thuc t6 chuc thuc hien cong viec, neu chua dat ta

thuc hién hai vong tiép theo.

![Page 136](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/329ea1d1-2da9-421a-8f4b-9c2aff4e282d.png)

---

- Vong 3: quay lai buoc 2 nghia la thay d6i phan chia cong viéc, thay d6i s6 t6 tho, thay d6i muc do chuyen mon h6a cong viéc. Neu chua dat ta chuyen sang vong 4.

- Vong 4 : quay lai buoc 1 - nghia la bat dau lai cong viec lap ke hoach san xuat, c6 su thay doi mot phan cong nghe thi cong. Thay d6i mot so cong nghe co the rut ngan thoi gian thi cong hoac nguoc lai de dat muc tiéu de ra. Khi phai thay doi lai cong nghe thi cong co nghia la ta phai tim mot bien phap thi cong khac. Khi d6 tat ca phai lam lai tu dau.

Tuy nhien cac vong 1, 2, 3, 4 se duoc thuc hien lan luot va quay nhiéu vong. Chi khi nao khong giai quyet duoc ? vong nay moi chuyen sang vong sau. Vi tinh phuc tap tang dan theo vong dieu chinh.

- Buoc 9: de de quan sat ta chuyen SDM sang truc thdi gian de phuc vu nhieu muc dich tiep theo.
- Buoc 10: khi SDM ta lap da dat cac tiéu chi de ra nhung van con du tru nhiéu kha nang hoan thien duoc ta tién hanh toi uu n6. Thong thuong nguoi ta su dung cac loai du tru de nang cao cac chi so ma ngudi xay dung mong muon. Hien nhien khi toi uu SDM khong duoc lam thay d6i cac tieu chi theo chieu bat loi.
- Buoc 1l: de tien cho viec su dung nhat la trong truong hop dieu hanh tien do tren bieu do nguoi ta chuyen SDM sang dang bieu do ngang. Trén bieu do ngang ta them mot s6 thong tin de ngudi su dung de dang nhan biet qua truc giac.
- Buoc 12: lap bieu d6 cung ung tai nguyén giong nhu cac cach da trinh bay dam bao tien do thuc thi nhu ké hoach. Cac buoc 9, 10, 11 se trinh bay cu the hon ? cac muc tiep theo.

Vi du: lap SDM gom cac cong viéc co thoi gian thuc hien va trinh tu thuc hien (trinh tu cong nghe va to chuc) nhu bang duoi (bang 4.2):

## 4.5.3. Dy tru thoi gian cua cong viec

Mbi cong viec ij bi gioi han boi hai su kien dau i va cuoi j. Do sy xuat hien cua su kien co the thay d6i tao cho cong viec co khoang du tru thoi gian.

Du tru la khoang thoi gian cong viéc co the thay doi su bat dau hay keo dai thoi gian thi cong trong pham vi co the. Can cu vao pham vi co the nguoi ta chia du tru ra bon loai nhu sau (hinh 4.12)

## 4.6. TiNH TOAN SDM

Tinh tay voi SDM c6 s6 su kién nh? hon 300, tinh may voi s0 su kién lon hon 300. Tinh toan SDM tuc la tinh toan tat ca cac thong so cua no.

Co hai phuong phap tinh toan: tinh truc tiep tren SDM (tinh theo su kien) va tinh theo bang (tinh theo cong viec)


## 1. Tinh truc tiep tren SDM

Trinh tu tinh toan:

- Chia vong tron su kien ra bon phan (de the hien tinh va ghi ket qua tinh toan) (hinh 4.13).

Goc phan tu ben trai: ghi thoi diem xuat hien som cua su kien.

Goc phan tu ben phai: ghi thoi diem xuat hien muon cua su kien.

Goc phan tu phia tren: ghi so thu tu cua su kien.

## 4.7. DUONG GANG, Y NGHiA CUA DUONG GANG

Duong gang la duong dai nhat trong SDM di tu su kien kh?i cong dau den su uo tya uy oua 1i u sunp rp nno op oa 'yu uo qun uoy u cong trinh. Cac cong viec thuoc duong gang khong co du tru thoi gian. Khi mot cong viec tren duong gang vi ly do nao do bi cham tré thi thoi han hoan thanh cong trinh bi cham theo. Muon hoan thanh cong trinh dung thoi han

thi phai tap trung, uu tien cho cac cong viec thuoc duong gang.

p op ugu anu 1yd en qu uoo uoo nn u uiu 4ma uon 1 duong gang. Trong SDM co the co nhieu duong gang va nhung duong can gang nen khi rut ngan thoi han thi cong phai chu y den tat ca nhung duong nay.


## 4.8. SDM TrEN TRUC THOI GIAN

Muc dich chuyen SDM sang truc thoi gian la de de ve va dieu chinh cac bieu d6 tai nguyen, dong thoi de quan ly tien do.

Ban dau thong thuong co 2 truong hop chuyen ve truc thdi gian:

## 1. Cac cong viec deu bat dau som

Trinh tu:

- Chon truc thoi gian Ot.
- Dua cac cong viéc gang ve truc thoi gian dat cac su kien gang ? dung vi tri theo thoi diem T' ve duoc cong viec bang cach noi cac su kién lai.
- Dua cac cong viec khong gang ve truc thoi gian.

Dat cac su kien ? moc hoan thanh som Ts sau d6 ve mui ten cong viéc bat dau tu su kien dau. Chieu dai mui ten cong viec bang thdi gian thuc hién cong viec (ki hieu bang doan thang hai net song song) cong voi du tru riéng cua cong viec (ki hieu bang doan net manh).

- Tren cong viéc ghi thoi gian thuc hien va tai nguyen tuong ung.

- a) Lap truc tOy: truc hoanh bieu dien thoi gian thuc hien cong viec. Truc tung bieu dien thu tu cong viec. Phia trai truc toa do Oy lap cac cot ghi thong so can thiet cac cong viec nhu thu tu,.ten, tai nguyen .. (hinh 4.17)

3. Cac cong viec dugc dat theo thu tu tang dan chi so su kien dau cong viéc. Neu co nhieu cong viec co cung su kien bat dau thi duoc dat theo su tang dan cua chi so su kién ket thuc cong viec.

Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS

- Moi cong viec duoc xac dinh b?i hai toa do la: hoanh do dau mut trai (thoi diem bat dau cong viec) va hoanh do dau mut phai (thoi diem ket thuc cong viéc).

Chu y : hoanh do dau mut trai cua mot cong viéc bang hoanh do dau mut phai cua cong viéc lién truoc. Neu co nhieu cap viec lien truoc thi hoanh do dau mut trai cua cong viec can bieu dien dudc dat trung voi hoanh do dau mut phai cua cong viéc lien truoc nao ? xa goc toa do nhat so v6i cong viec kia (dat theo hoanh do lon nhat cua cong viec lién truoc).

Hoanh do dau mut phai cua mot cong viéc bang hoanh do dau mut trai cong thoi gian thuc hién.

- Duong gang

(hoanh do d6 chinh la chieu dai duong gang). ## 4.10. NHUNG BAI TOAN TOI UU SO dO MANG

Lap tien do bang SDM co tinh uu viet ma cac phuong phap khac kho ma dat duoc d6 la sau khi lap duoc mot tien do phu hop voi cong nghe thi cong nguoi ta con co the lam cho tien do dat duoc nhung chi so tot hon nua. Qua trinh dua tien do dat duoc nhung pham chat tot dep hon, mang lai hieu qua cho to chuic san xuat ta goi la toi uu hoa hay cu the hon la toi uu hoa so d6 mang.

Khi thue hien toi uu hoa SDM ngudi ta thuong dua ra nhung muc dich cu the. Cac muc dich nay thuong duoc bieu hien bang nhung dieu kien han che. Mot trong nhung dieu kien thuong gap trong lap ke hoach (tien do) la su dung tai nguyen liéen tuc, dieu hoa va dua cong trinh vao su dung dung ke hoach dat chat ludng xay lap cao, gia thanh thap.

Tuy nhien van de toi uu hoa tien do la bai toan co mien xac dinh rong va phuc tap. Kho co bai toan nao co the giai quyet nhieu muc tiéu cung mot luc. Trong chung muc cu the nguoi ta dua ra cac bai toan voi nhung chi tieu han che. Nhung bai toan toi uu tien do (SDM) thuong gap la:

- 1) Bai toan dieu chinh thoi han thi cong theo ke hoach dinh truoc
- 2) Bai toan phan phoi tai nguyen thu hoi theo thoi gian da dinh
- 3) Bai toan thoi gian va gia thanh cong trinh
- 4) Bai toan phan phoi tai nguyén sao cho thoi giar: thi cong la ngan nhat.
- 5) Bai toan xac dinh thoi han xay dung cong trinh toi uu.

Sau day ta xem xet cu the mot so trong nhung bai toan d6.

## 2. Dieu chinh SDM vé thoi gian nhan luc

Khi SDM da dat tieu chuan thoi gian (L, ≤ Tp) nhung bieu do nhan luc chua dat cac tiéu chi de ra. Ta can lam theo cach sau:

- Tim tren biéu do nhan luc nhung khoang c6 nhan luc tang hoac giam dot ngot.
- Tim cac cong viec nam trong thoi gian tuong ung voi khoang thoi gian co bieu do khong tot noi tren.
- Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS Giam hoac tang nhan luc cho cac cong viec d6 sao cho dat duoc doan nhan luc hop ly. Hoac co the xe dich cac cong viéc do (thay d6i thdi han bat dau) hoac keo dai thdi gian thuc hien cong viec (voi dieu kien khong vuot qua thoi gian du tru) sao cho dat dudc muc dich la: cho nhan luc up buea oonp weia op aunh guo 'atyn ueo op suna uap wei oonp su tung do can thiet. Cu dieu chinh nhu vay cho den khi dat duoc su diéu

## 4.12. tO1 UU HOa SDM THEO CHi TIEU THOI GIAN - CHI PHi

Chi phi trong xay dung gom hai loai: truc tiep phi va gian tiép phi. Moi quan he giua truc tiep phi voi thoi gian thi cong cua cong viec duoc bieu dien nhu hinh 4.20a

voi thoi gian thi cong khan truong nhat).

Diem B: cong viec thi cong voi thoi gian binh thuong, chi phi la nho nhat C

Vuot qua diem B: chi phi truc tiep lai tang lén. Moi quan he giua gian tiep phi voi thoi gian thi cong duoc bieu dien tren hinh 4.20b

Thoi gian thi cong nh? nhat cho chi phi gian tiep nh? nhat. Thoi gian thi cong kéo dai thi chi phi gian tiep cang tang. Duong cong chi phi tong cong - thoi gian thi cong (duong C) bieu dien tren hinh 4.20c

## 4.13. PHAN PHOl VA SU DUNG TAI NGUYEN tOI UU TRONG LAP Ke HOaCh va Chi daO SAn xuAT

![Page 171](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/9d3dfe81-e832-477c-aafd-47eafbbbd768.png)

---

aunp dex uey su o nn up A nyo saa you as suea op uan dei cong trinh, nguoi ta con chu y den van dé phan phoi va su dung tai nguyén cho san xuat. Vi ngoai viec hoan chinh cong trinh dung thoi han can phai chu y den hieu qua kinh te cua phuong an t6 chuc thi cong. Tai nguyen su dung cho san xuat xay dung gom nhieu loai khac nhau. Chung co the la nhan luc lao dong, can bo ky thuat, may moc thiet bi, vat lieu xay dung, ket cau che tao truoc, nang luong, tai chinh... Khi lam viec voi SDM nguoi ta thuong xay dung d6 la no co hay khong thay doi khoi luong khi duoc su dung.

Theo tieu chi nay nguoi ta chia tai nguyén ra lam hai loai: tai nguyen thu hoi dugc va tai nguyen khong thu hoi duoc sau si dung.

Tai nguyen thu hoi la tai nguyen khong bien doi so luong trong qua trinh su dung. Trong loai nay gom nhan luc, can bo ky thuat, may moc, thiet bi, co s? san xuat phu tro. Ngudi ta con goi loai nay la tai nguyen khong xep kho duoc. S6 luong cua chung khong thay doi trong qua trinh san xuat, no chi thay doi khi co su trao doi voi cac don vi xay lap khac.

Dieu kien rang buoc cua tai nguyen thu hoi trong phan phoi cho san xuat la:

<!-- formula-not-decoded -->

trong do: R;(t) - cuong do su dung tai nguyen i tai thoi diem t;

H,(t) - khoi luong hien co cua tai nguyén i tai thoi diem t.

Y nghia cua cong thuc (4.23) la khong lap ke hoach tiéu thu tai nguyen vuot qua muc hien co cua don vi thi cong.

Loai thu hai la tai nguyen thay doi khoi luong trong qua trinh su dung. Khoi luong bien d6i ti lée thuan voi khoi luong cong viéc hoan thanh (khong ke den moi su hao mon trong bao quan, van chuyen), do bien thanh san pham. Trong nhom nay dien hinh la vat lieu, nhien lieu ban thanh pham, cau kién va dac biet la tién von.

Moi cong viec su dung mot vai loai tai nguyen khac nhau, mat khac viec su dung nhieu loai tai dung cua mbi loai cung khac nhau lam cho bai toan su dung toi uu tai nguyen cang tr? nen phuc tap. Khi giai bai toan them mot loai tai nguyen thi do phuc tap tang lén gap boi, neu nhieu doi khi khong giai noi. Trong thuc te nguoi ta o h e oo os d d o o san xuat la du. Mot trong nhuing cach d6 la:

Chon trong nhung tai nguyen lien quan nhung tai nguyen co y nghia nhat. Trong nhung tai nguyen chon ra tim tai nguyen chu dao. Giai bai toan voi tai uoo uaknau 1en bunqu up da aax gp ps po wei li 1oi &lt;el oep nyo uaanau theo thu tu uu tien (muc quan trong trong san xuat).

Trong san xuat xay dung tai nguyen chu dao thuong la nhan luc (lao dong song) nen kem theo mot tien do xay dung hop ly phai co bieu do nhan luc toi uu.

Cung phai chu y la trong san xuat rat nhieu loai tai nguyen cuong do su dung luon luon song hanh va ti le thuan voi tai nguyen chu dao, vi du: nhu cau ve su dung vat tu, trang thiet bi san xuat ti lé voi bieu do nhan luc d6 la dieu de hieu vi ngudi dong se san xuat nhieu nen phai dung nhieu thiet bi dung cu va vat tu de san xuat.

## 5.1. KhAI NIEm VE SO dO mANG nUT

So do mang truyen thong trinh bay trong chuong IV la so d6 mang cong viec the hien bang cung con su kien the hien bang cac nut. Tuy phuong phap so d6 mang truyen thong co nhieu uu diem nhung con mot so nhuoc diem la phuong phap the hien con qua chi tiet va ruom ra, tinh mem deo chua duoc cao. Nhat la nhung so d6 mang co nhieu su kien (cong viec) viec tinh toan va dieu hanh co nhung han che. Hien ? cac nuoc phuong Tay (Anh, My, Phap...) uanau ga 'oaia suo anu sueu gp os yeoi aou uata gyd gyy sunp ns e tonau ly co ban cung nhu so d6 mang truyen thong nhung khac phuc dugc nhung nhuoc diem ? tren lam cho so d6 mang nay mem déo hon dé phu hop voi thuc te san xuat hon.

De phan biet hai loai sd d6 mang, loai truyen thong ta goi la so do mang su kién. Con so d6 mang nut cong viec ta goi la so d6 mang cong viec hay ngan gon hon la so do mang nut.

Nhu vay, so d6 mang (SDM) la danh tu dung chung cho cac phuong phap so do mang.

![Page 180](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/76355fb7-362f-4d03-87ea-4e0296b8f019.png)

---

SDM su kien la SDM trong do nut the hien su kien.

SDM cong viec la SDM trong do nut the hien cong viec goi tat la SDM nut.

Trong tai lieu nay ti day ve sau chung ta se dung hai danh ti SDM su kien va SDM nut.


## 5.2. THE HIEN SO dO MANG NUT

Trong so do mang nut cac nut the hien mot cong viec. Giua cac cong viec the hién su phu thuoc lan nhau. Nut cua mang the hien cong viec c6 dang hinh chu nhat, trong d6 ghi cac thong s6 (hinh 5.1) cua cong viec. Tuy theo muc no oa suo u uo a gs uo oo g as dei nu eno yo Trung taâm ñaøo taïo xaây döïng VIETCONS

![Image](images/docling_102.jpg)

nhau. Nhung cac thong so chu yeu (ten cong viec i, thoi gian thuc hien t, thoi diem kh?i som Kh', thoi diem kh?i muon Kh") deu phai co. Ngoai ra, nguoi ta R, su dung vat liéu, may moc, du tru (z) cua cong viéc.

Hinh 5.4. Sy tuong dong gitra hai loai SDM

![Image](images/docling_103.jpg)

![Page 181](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/67738ba9-f661-4476-bf51-c10855c3c35b.png)

---

a) Mang sy kien; b) Mang nut.

Moi quan he giua cac cong viec trong SDM nut the hien bang cac mui ten, bao gom chieu mui ten va gia tri thoi gian phu thuoc do. Thong thuong nguoi ta co the the hien lien he giua mot thoi diém bat ky (a) cua cong viec nay voi mot thoi diem (b) cua cong viéc kia (hinh 5.2).

Diem lién he a va b thuong lay theo so % khoi luong cong viec da hoan thanh, con thoi gian lién he la dieu kien rang buoc.

Vi du tren hinh 5.2 cong viec A phai hoan thanh 30% khoi luong cong viéc truoc 7 ngay cong viec B hoan thanh 20% khoi luong cong viec.

Trong thuc te ta thuong gap cac diem lien he la luc bat dau (S) va ket thuc (F). Tren hinh 5.3 the hien cac lien he do.

Cac moi lien he de dang chuyen doi ti quan he nay sang quan he khac, ph6 bien nhat la SS va FS. Chieu mui ten co the thuan tu cong viec truoc sang cong viec sau the hien moi lien he khong som hon va chieu nghich - the hien khong muon hon.

Gia tri phu thuoc t khong som hon mang dau duong (+) va khong muon hon mang dau (-).

Voi cach trinh bay nhu vay tren SDM nut khong co viec ao va nhung viéc lam song song goi tiep khong phai tach ra thanh nhieu viec nhu SDM su kien.

Tuy nhién, giua hai loai SDM su kien va nut luon co su tuong dong co the sun gs ao un gun tg yuy u '1iy 1on us Aeu rio 2 ip uonnyo hop cu the.

![Page 182](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/31758990-b642-4634-93bc-257beae3f73b.png)

## 4. SDM nut co nhieu moi lien he

O muc a, b, c cung muc trinh bay cac truong hop tinh toan SDM thuong gap. Cac truong hop con lai trong d6 c6 truong hop day di cac moi lien hé ve ly thuyet van co the co. Khi gap cac truong hop co nhieu moi quan hé truoc khi tinh toan ta can loai bo nhung moi lien he trai nguoc nhau (lien he nao khong hop ly loai bo). Sau do moi thuc hien tinh toan.

![Page 196](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/134d20a3-8bc4-4198-a84b-1455c1c4e7e7.png)

---

Cach tinh toan cung tuong tu nhu da trinh bay tren day.


## 5.4. CHUYEN SDM NUT SANG SO DO NGANG

De tien cho viec theo doi, dieu hanh, cac tien do lap theo SDM nut dudc chuyen sang sd d6 ngang (Gantt). Khi dung chuong trinh may tinh nguoi ta cung thuong the hien ket qua tren so d6 ngang khong qua giai doan the hien mang nut.

## 5.5. SO DO MANG PERT

So do mang la mot hinh thuc the hien dong bo cac cong viec can thiet de thi cong xay dung cong trinh. Khi phan tich SDM nho co nhung thong so cho ta biet tinh hinh thi cong cong trinh de tim cach lap ke hoach va chi dao thuc hien.

Mot trong nhung thong s6 chu yeu trong SDM la thoi han hoan thanh cac cong viec (t;). Chat luong cia SDM va hieu qua cua viéc quan ly dieu hanh tien do phu thuoc rat nhieu vao do chinh xac cua thong so thdi gian. Thong so6 thoi gian thuc hien cac cong viec chinh xac cang cao thi SDM cang sat thuc te, cac bien phap dieu hanh thi cong cang co hieu qua.

Trong cac muc truoc chung ta da nghien cuu SDM voi cac thong so thoi gian hoach xay dung nguoi ta thudng dua tren cac dinh muc. Tuy nhién, trong dinh muc chi co nhung cong viec nhung chi phi thoi gian dat do chinh xac cao, con nhung cong viéc dieu kién thi cong phu thuoc nhieu yeu t6 khach quan nen tinh xac dinh rat thap (dinh muc khong phan anh day du).

![Page 198](/static/doc-images/kb_13/images/6b5a7fb8-1688-446a-b7f9-274f49652795.png)

---

Mot khi ke hoach bao gom nhung yeu to khong xac dinh thi viéc hoan thanh dung du dinh co tinh ngau nhien. De danh gia kha nang hoan thanh ke hoach nguoi ta dua vao phuong phap phan bo xac suat. D6 la noi dung chu yeu cua phuong phap SDM "P.E.R.T" (Program Evaluation and Review Technique).

Ban chat cua phuong phap PERT la dua yeu t6 khong xac dinh vao uoc luong khong du du kien de nguoi ta xac dinh thdi han hoan thanh chinh xac dan den viec hoan thanh du an cung co do tin cay khac nhau.

Phuong phap PERT lay thoi gian trung binh mong muon (t) kem theo mot dai luong do do khong xac dinh cua thoi gian thuc hien cong viec vao lap ke hoach va danh gia kha nang hoan thanh du an. Dai luong d6 la do lech chuan

(oe) hay phuong sai (v.) cua thoi gian trung binh mong muon.

Thoi gian trung binh mong muon la thoi gian uoc luong c6 khoang 50% kha nang thuc hién som hon va 50% kha nang thuc hien muon hon. De xac dinh gia tri d6 moi cong viec can phai co ham phan bo xac suat thuc hien. Do khong co thong tin ve phan bo xac suat thoi gian thuc hién cong viec vi n6 co nhieu bien dong ngau nhien va kéo dai nen nguoi ta phai gia thiet mot ham phan bo phu hop voi tüng cong viéc.

Dua tren uoc luong nhung gia tri thoi gian nam trong duong cong ly thuyet cho phép suy ra bang toan hoc gia tri trung binh mong muon voi do léch chuan va phuong sai cua no (hinh 5.11)


## Ba gia tri do la :

Thoi gian lac quan ta : la thoi gian cong viec hoan thanh som nhat khi gap dieu kien dac biet thuan loi.

Thdi gian co xac suat cao nhat t,: la thoi han hoan thanh cong viec co nhieu kha nang xay ra nhat.

---

Thdi gian bi quan t, : la thdi gian toi da (muon nhat) hoan thanh cong viéc khi gap dieu kién kho khan nhat.
